--- SKILL ---
---
name: gitnexus-pr-review
description: "Use when the user wants to review a pull request, understand what a PR changes, assess risk of merging, or check for missing test coverage. Examples: \"Review this PR\", \"What does PR #42 change?\", \"Is this PR safe to merge?\""
---

# PR Review with GitNexus

## When to Use

- "Review this PR"
- "What does PR #42 change?"
- "Is this safe to merge?"
- "What's the blast radius of this PR?"
- "Are there missing tests for this PR?"
- Reviewing someone else's code changes before merge

## Workflow

```
1. gh pr diff <number>                                    → Get the raw diff
2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})  → Map diff to affected flows
3. For each changed symbol:
   gitnexus_impact({target: "<symbol>", direction: "upstream"})    → Blast radius per change
4. gitnexus_context({name: "<key symbol>"})               → Understand callers/callees
5. READ gitnexus://repo/{name}/processes                   → Check affected execution flows
6. Summarize findings with risk assessment
```

> If "Index is stale" → run `npx gitnexus analyze` in terminal before reviewing.

## Checklist

```
- [ ] Fetch PR diff (gh pr diff or git diff base...head)
- [ ] gitnexus_detect_changes to map changes to affected execution flows
- [ ] gitnexus_impact on each non-trivial changed symbol
- [ ] Review d=1 items (WILL BREAK) — are callers updated?
- [ ] gitnexus_context on key changed symbols to understand full picture
- [ ] Check if affected processes have test coverage
- [ ] Assess overall risk level
- [ ] Write review summary with findings
```

## Review Dimensions

| Dimension | How GitNexus Helps |
| --- | --- |
| **Correctness** | `context` shows callers — are they all compatible with the change? |
| **Blast radius** | `impact` shows d=1/d=2/d=3 dependents — anything missed? |
| **Completeness** | `detect_changes` shows all affected flows — are they all handled? |
| **Test coverage** | `impact({includeTests: true})` shows which tests touch changed code |
| **Breaking changes** | d=1 upstream items that aren't updated in the PR = potential breakage |

## Risk Assessment

| Signal | Risk |
| --- | --- |
| Changes touch <3 symbols, 0-1 processes | LOW |
| Changes touch 3-10 symbols, 2-5 processes | MEDIUM |
| Changes touch >10 symbols or many processes | HIGH |
| Changes touch auth, payments, or data integrity code | CRITICAL |
| d=1 callers exist outside the PR diff | Potential breakage — flag it |

## Tools

**gitnexus_detect_changes** — map PR diff to affected execution flows:

```
gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})

→ Changed: 8 symbols in 4 files
→ Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow, WebhookHandler
→ Risk: MEDIUM
```

**gitnexus_impact** — blast radius per changed symbol:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})

→ d=1 (WILL BREAK):
  - processCheckout (src/checkout.ts:42) [CALLS, 100%]
  - webhookHandler (src/webhooks.ts:15) [CALLS, 100%]

→ d=2 (LIKELY AFFECTED):
  - checkoutRouter (src/routes/checkout.ts:22) [CALLS, 95%]
```

**gitnexus_impact with tests** — check test coverage:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream", includeTests: true})

→ Tests that cover this symbol:
  - validatePayment.test.ts [direct]
  - checkout.integration.test.ts [via processCheckout]
```

**gitnexus_context** — understand a changed symbol's role:

```
gitnexus_context({name: "validatePayment"})

→ Incoming calls: processCheckout, webhookHandler
→ Outgoing calls: verifyCard, fetchRates
→ Processes: CheckoutFlow (step 3/7), RefundFlow (step 1/5)
```

## Example: "Review PR #42"

```
1. gh pr diff 42 > /tmp/pr42.diff
   → 4 files changed: payments.ts, checkout.ts, types.ts, utils.ts

2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})
   → Changed symbols: validatePayment, PaymentInput, formatAmount
   → Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow
   → Risk: MEDIUM

3. gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: processCheckout, webhookHandler (WILL BREAK)
   → webhookHandler is NOT in the PR diff — potential breakage!

4. gitnexus_impact({target: "PaymentInput", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: validatePayment (in PR), createPayment (NOT in PR)
   → createPayment uses the old PaymentInput shape — breaking change!

5. gitnexus_context({name: "formatAmount"})
   → Called by 12 functions — but change is backwards-compatible (added optional param)

6. Review summary:
   - MEDIUM risk — 3 changed symbols affect 2 execution flows
   - BUG: webhookHandler calls validatePayment but isn't updated for new signature
   - BUG: createPayment depends on PaymentInput type which changed
   - OK: formatAmount change is backwards-compatible
   - Tests: checkout.test.ts covers processCheckout path, but no webhook test
```

## Review Output Format

Structure your review as:

```markdown
## PR Review: <title>

**Risk: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH / CRITICAL**

### Changes Summary
- <N> symbols changed across <M> files
- <P> execution flows affected

### Findings
1. **[severity]** Description of finding
   - Evidence from GitNexus tools
   - Affected callers/flows

### Missing Coverage
- Callers not updated in PR: ...
- Untested flows: ...

### Recommendation
APPROVE / REQUEST CHANGES / NEEDS DISCUSSION
```

 # Plexco — Setup dev · staging · production
 
 > **Version:** v2.1 · 2026-07-16
 > **Authority:** DD-04 = DigitalOcean co-location: App Platform + Managed PostgreSQL HA in Singapore,
 > connected through the `sgp1` VPC. The accepted record is
 > [`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`](../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md).
 > **Current repository state:** the local Docker Compose definition exists and validates; Docker-daemon
 > availability remains machine state and **must be checked before each run**. The daemon was started for the
 > 2026-07-16 closure and the canonical PostgreSQL/RLS/auth suite passed; the test container was then stopped.
 > The production DigitalOcean App Spec and deployment workflows are still an infra-slice deliverable.
 > `infra/fly.toml` is a bounded fallback artifact, not the production runbook.
 
 Plexco is a money-safe, multi-tenant system. Environment separation, DB-role separation, immutable promotion,
 and restore/failover drills are release gates rather than optional hardening.
 
 ---
 
 ## 0. Target environment model
 
 ```text
 local development
   docker compose + local PostgreSQL
             │
             │ build one immutable OCI image (release tag + verified digest)
             ▼
 ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
   production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
   sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
             │
             │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
             ▼
 production on DigitalOcean
   2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
 ```
 
 | Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 | **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
 | **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
 | **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
 | **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
 
 Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
 production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
 automatically after the TTL.
 
 ---
 
 ## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
 
 ### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
 
-DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. Record
-written evidence for every production dependency before creating the App Spec:
+DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
+table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
+and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
+`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
+contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
 
-| Dependency | Required answer |
-|---|---|
-| SePay | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Inbound webhook verification alone does not imply that it does. |
-| ApiPay | Does enterprise provisioning restrict API callers by fixed source IP? |
-| Zalo Bot | Does the send API require fixed outbound IP? |
-| Meta Messenger | Does the Graph API require fixed outbound IP? |
-
-- All answers **no** → continue with App Platform + VPC.
-- Any answer **yes** → stop. Do not disable the private DB path silently. Activate the bounded Fly+DO
-  fallback or design a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
+| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
+| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
+| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
+
+**Gate status: `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`** — SePay, ApiPay and Zalo Bot are `UNRESOLVED` (2026-07-18). No
+overall result is recorded and the closure marker is intentionally absent: their official documentation never
+affirmatively addresses outbound caller source-IP policy, and inference from omitted registration steps does
+not meet this gate's evidence bar. The owner must obtain an attributable support or contractual response from
+each of the three providers (for ApiPay: the DD-30 enterprise onboarding contract explicitly, not only the
+BYO API) before this gate can close.
+
+- All four rows attributable and **NO** → record `Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` plus the closure marker.
+  That permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review only**; it does
+  not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning.
+- All four rows attributable and one or more **YES** → record `Overall result: FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED`. That
+  blocks the DO App Platform + VPC green path and requires a separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO
+  fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+- Any row still missing, ambiguous, stale, or only inferred → gate status stays `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`;
+  no overall result or closure marker may be recorded, even if another row is already an attributable `YES`.
+- Until the gate closes, do not create the App Spec and do not disable the private DB path silently.
 
 ### 1.2 Repository preconditions
 
 Before the infra slice runs:
 
 ```bash
 test -f infra/Dockerfile
 test -f infra/entrypoint.sh
 test -f .github/workflows/ci.yml
 test -f infra/app.prod.yaml       # created and reviewed by the infra slice
 test -f infra/app.staging.yaml    # created and reviewed by the infra slice
 ```
 
 If either App Spec is absent, this runbook is a deployment contract only: **do not improvise a console-only
 production setup**. IaC must remain the reproducible source.
 
 ---
 
 ## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
 
 Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
 Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
 
 | Resource | Staging | Production |
 |---|---|---|
 | App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
 | App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
 | VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
 | PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
 | DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
 | Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
 | Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
 
 Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
 ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.
 
 ---
 
 ## 3. One image, three App Platform components
 
 The App Specs deploy the same immutable image as three components. Use a unique release tag such as the git SHA,
 resolve and record its registry digest, and prohibit mutable `latest` promotion.
 
 | Component | Day-1 production shape | Command | DB identity | Scaling contract |
 |---|---:|---|---|---|
 | `web` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh web` | `app` pooled URL + `app` direct listener URL | Request-based RPS/p95 autoscaling is eligible on shared CPU; CPU autoscaling requires dedicated CPU |
 | `discovery` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh discovery` | `readonly` pooled URL on the primary at Day 1 | Bounded pool + timeout + CDN + circuit breaker; read-only node only after telemetry trigger |
 | `worker` | `1 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb-fixed` | `infra/entrypoint.sh worker` | `app_worker` direct/session URL | One durable consumer at Day 1; add a second scalable worker only after idempotency and queue-lag gates pass |
 
 App Platform high availability for the HTTP paths requires at least two containers, hence `web=2` and
 `discovery=2`. The worker starts single by design; pg-boss durability is in PostgreSQL, not local disk.
 
 ### Required App Spec controls
 
 - VPC attachment to the environment's `sgp1` VPC; no dedicated egress-IP block on the VPC path.
 - Set `TRUSTED_INGRESS_PROVIDER=digitalocean` on every public HTTP component. Application rate limits trust
   only DigitalOcean's overwritten `do-connecting-ip`; they never fall back to `X-Forwarded-For` or a header
   owned by another provider. The bounded Fly fallback must instead set `fly`; `direct` is local-only.
 - Health checks on `/healthz` for both HTTP services.
 - Separate component-level environment variables and secrets.
 - Explicit instance size/count; no console-only drift.
 - Image release tag/digest recorded in deployment evidence.
 - Deployment-failure and domain-failure alerts.
 - TTL metadata and destruction job for staging.
 
 ---
 
 ## 4. Database roles and connection variables
 
 Create four roles per database. The migration/owner credential is never injected into an App Platform runtime
 component.
 
 | Role | Runtime surface | Permissions | Connection mode |
 |---|---|---|---|
 | `migration` | CI deployment job only | DDL, migrations, grants, controlled queue provisioning | Direct; job-scoped secret |
 | `app` | `web` | Tenant RLS, application functions; `NOSUPERUSER NOBYPASSRLS` | Transaction pool for requests; separate direct connection for `LISTEN` |
 | `readonly` | `discovery` | `SELECT` only on `public_sessions` and approved public views; zero grant on canonical money/PII tables | Bounded transaction pool |
 | `app_worker` | `worker` | Narrow worker functions, pg-boss/outbox/projector operations | Direct/session connection |
 
 The application contract intentionally stays provider-neutral:
@@ -197,154 +213,155 @@ pull request
 App creation/update is automated from reviewed specs:
 
 ```bash
 # First create; use `doctl apps update` for later reconciliations.
 doctl apps create --spec infra/app.staging.yaml
 doctl apps create --spec infra/app.prod.yaml
 ```
 
 The CI workflow supplies the immutable release value to both specs. Do not rebuild for production and do not
 promote a mutable tag.
 
 ### Required CI order
 
 1. Validate target PostgreSQL version/extensions and roles.
 2. Run migrations with `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION`.
 3. Run `infra/entrypoint.sh provision` using the same job-scoped credential.
 4. Deploy/reconcile components.
 5. Wait for health/readiness; execute smoke and reconnect checks.
 
 Migration and queue provisioning must not be implemented as an App Platform runtime command that injects the DDL
 credential into all components.
 
 ---
 
 ## 7. URLs and channel configuration
 
 Use distinct domains and provider registrations:
 
 | Surface | Local/integration | Staging | Production |
 |---|---|---|---|
 | Google OAuth redirect | local/tunnel client | staging client + staging domain | production client + production domain |
 | Meta Login/Messenger | developer/test app or test users | staging app/page | live reviewed app/page |
 | Zalo | test club/bot | staging club/bot | per-club production tokens encrypted in DB |
 | SePay/ApiPay | sandbox/test account | staging account | production accounts/webhooks |
 | PWA/API base | localhost/tunnel | staging domain | production domain |
 
 Webhook updates are idempotent deployment steps. Verify the registered callback after every domain/app change; do
 not rely on a manual checklist entry without provider-side evidence.
 
 ---
 
 ## 8. Rollback and recovery
 
 ### Application rollback
 
 - Select the previously verified immutable release in the App Spec and reconcile the app.
 - Confirm `web`/`discovery` health, SSE re-subscription, worker queue lag and no duplicate money mutations.
 - If a forward migration has run, do not execute destructive down-migrations. Use expand/contract and forward-fix.
 
 ### Database recovery
 
 - Provider PITR and HA are not sufficient alone. Maintain encrypted logical exports outside the DO account.
 - Quarterly, restore to vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema/role/RLS/sequence checks plus
   ledger, allocation, outbox and row-count reconciliation.
 - Rehearse the cross-provider flow and rollback semantics from DD-04 §7.4. Never dual-write money data during
   migration and never point back to a stale source after the target has accepted writes.
 
 ---
 
 ## 9. Ephemeral staging lifecycle
 
 Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
 
 1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
 2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
 3. runs the selected gates;
 4. exports drill evidence;
 5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
 6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
 
 Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
 silently become an always-on production mirror.
 
 ---
 
 ## 10. Production acceptance checklist
 
 This list mirrors the accepted record §7.6 and must be represented as executable checks/evidence in the infra
 slice:
 
-- [ ] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence.
+- [ ] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence — still open: §1.1 gate status
+      `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE` (SePay/ApiPay/Zalo Bot unresolved, checked 2026-07-18).
 - [ ] App Platform `sgp` is attached to the intended `sgp1` VPC.
 - [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only required private identities.
 - [ ] Four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener URLs work over the private route with TLS verification.
 - [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` pass rolling-deploy and health tests.
 - [ ] Discovery pool cap, `statement_timeout`, reserved money connection budget, CDN/rate limits and circuit breaker pass load tests.
 - [ ] Primary-failure drill proves DB failover and application reconnect.
 - [ ] SSE re-subscribes and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
 - [ ] PITR and off-provider restores meet measured RPO/RTO and reconciliation gates.
 - [ ] KMS rotation and decrypt-failure paths pass.
 - [ ] Ephemeral staging reproduces from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
 - [ ] Load evidence captures RPS/concurrency, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate, egress and projected cost.
 - [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal and asymmetric rollback pass DD-04 §7.4.
 - [ ] Budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup and KMS.
 
 ---
 
 ## 11. Cost planning
 
 | Environment | Planning envelope |
 |---|---:|
 | Local/integration smoke | `$0–15/month`, depending on whether a shared public callback target is kept |
 | Ephemeral staging | `$8–18/month` average at 48–96h per release/drill |
 | Production Day 1 | **`$120–135/month`** |
 | Production + expected staging | **`$128–153/month`** |
 
 The production baseline includes two `web`, two `discovery`, one worker, DO PostgreSQL primary+standby,
 Cloudflare Free, Sentry Developer, KMS, low-volume logs/offsite backup and egress allowances. Physical read-only
 node, second worker, WAF Pro, Sentry Team and resizing are telemetry-triggered upgrades, not Day-1 requirements.
 
 Host count does not select a resource tier. Scale purchases require staging/production measurements and the
 triggers in DD-04 §5.2.
 
 ---
 
 ## 12. Bounded Fly fallback
 
 `infra/fly.toml` is retained only because DD-04 pre-authorizes fallback `(a)` when a production dependency proves
 that fixed outbound IP is mandatory and App Platform+VPC cannot satisfy it.
 
 Activating fallback requires a recorded gate result and a separate reviewed App/DB network plan:
 
 - compute on Fly with static egress;
 - DO PostgreSQL reached over public TLS with `sslmode=verify-full` and IP allowlist;
 - the same four DB roles, immutable image, migration-job boundary, backup/restore and acceptance gates;
 - a new cost reconciliation before procurement.
 
 Do not use the old “three Fly apps + Fly Postgres” topology. If fallback is not activated, no production Fly
 resource is provisioned.
 
 ---
 
 ## 13. Anti-patterns
 
 - One app/database or shared secrets across staging and production.
 - Console-only production resources that IaC cannot reproduce.
 - Migration/owner credential in any runtime component.
 - Mutable image tags or rebuilding between staging and production.
 - Treating the HA standby as the discovery replica.
 - Buying a read-only node from host count rather than measured contention.
 - Disabling VPC to obtain a fixed egress IP without activating the approved fallback/design branch.
 - Treating provider PITR as the off-provider backup.
 - Leaving ephemeral staging alive without an owner and TTL.
 
 ---

    10	> The production DigitalOcean App Spec and deployment workflows are still an infra-slice deliverable.
    11	> `infra/fly.toml` is a bounded fallback artifact, not the production runbook.
    12	
    13	Plexco is a money-safe, multi-tenant system. Environment separation, DB-role separation, immutable promotion,
    14	and restore/failover drills are release gates rather than optional hardening.

    18	## 0. Target environment model
    19	
    20	```text
    21	local development
    22	  docker compose + local PostgreSQL
    23	            │
    24	            │ build one immutable OCI image (release tag + verified digest)
    25	            ▼
    26	ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
    27	  production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
    28	  sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
    29	            │
    30	            │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
    31	            ▼
    32	production on DigitalOcean
    33	  2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
    34	```
    35	
    36	| Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
    37	|---|---|---|---|---|
    38	| **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
    39	| **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
    40	| **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
    41	| **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
    42	
    43	Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
    44	production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
    45	automatically after the TTL.

    49	## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
    50	
    51	### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
    52	
    53	DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
    54	table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
    55	and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
    56	`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
    57	contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
    58	
    59	| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
    60	|---|---|---|---|---|
    61	| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
    62	| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
    63	| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
    64	| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
    65	
    66	**Gate status: `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`** — SePay, ApiPay and Zalo Bot are `UNRESOLVED` (2026-07-18). No
    67	overall result is recorded and the closure marker is intentionally absent: their official documentation never
    68	affirmatively addresses outbound caller source-IP policy, and inference from omitted registration steps does
    69	not meet this gate's evidence bar. The owner must obtain an attributable support or contractual response from
    70	each of the three providers (for ApiPay: the DD-30 enterprise onboarding contract explicitly, not only the
    71	BYO API) before this gate can close.
    72	
    73	- All four rows attributable and **NO** → record `Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` plus the closure marker.
    74	  That permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review only**; it does
    75	  not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning.
    76	- All four rows attributable and one or more **YES** → record `Overall result: FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED`. That
    77	  blocks the DO App Platform + VPC green path and requires a separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO
    78	  fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
    79	- Any row still missing, ambiguous, stale, or only inferred → gate status stays `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`;
    80	  no overall result or closure marker may be recorded, even if another row is already an attributable `YES`.
    81	- Until the gate closes, do not create the App Spec and do not disable the private DB path silently.
    82	
    83	### 1.2 Repository preconditions
    84	
    85	Before the infra slice runs:
    86

    88	test -f infra/Dockerfile
    89	test -f infra/entrypoint.sh
    90	test -f .github/workflows/ci.yml
    91	test -f infra/app.prod.yaml       # created and reviewed by the infra slice
    92	test -f infra/app.staging.yaml    # created and reviewed by the infra slice
    93	```

    95	If either App Spec is absent, this runbook is a deployment contract only: **do not improvise a console-only
    96	production setup**. IaC must remain the reproducible source.

   100	## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
   101	
   102	Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
   103	Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
   104	
   105	| Resource | Staging | Production |
   106	|---|---|---|
   107	| App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
   108	| App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
   109	| VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
   110	| PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
   111	| DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
   112	| Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
   113	| Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
   114	
   115	Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
   116	ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.
   117

   120	## 3. One image, three App Platform components
   121	
   122	The App Specs deploy the same immutable image as three components. Use a unique release tag such as the git SHA,
   123	resolve and record its registry digest, and prohibit mutable `latest` promotion.
   124	
   125	| Component | Day-1 production shape | Command | DB identity | Scaling contract |
   126	|---|---:|---|---|---|
   127	| `web` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh web` | `app` pooled URL + `app` direct listener URL | Request-based RPS/p95 autoscaling is eligible on shared CPU; CPU autoscaling requires dedicated CPU |
   128	| `discovery` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh discovery` | `readonly` pooled URL on the primary at Day 1 | Bounded pool + timeout + CDN + circuit breaker; read-only node only after telemetry trigger |
   129	| `worker` | `1 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb-fixed` | `infra/entrypoint.sh worker` | `app_worker` direct/session URL | One durable consumer at Day 1; add a second scalable worker only after idempotency and queue-lag gates pass |
   130	
   131	App Platform high availability for the HTTP paths requires at least two containers, hence `web=2` and
   132	`discovery=2`. The worker starts single by design; pg-boss durability is in PostgreSQL, not local disk.
   133	
   134	### Required App Spec controls
   135	
   136	- VPC attachment to the environment's `sgp1` VPC; no dedicated egress-IP block on the VPC path.
   137	- Set `TRUSTED_INGRESS_PROVIDER=digitalocean` on every public HTTP component. Application rate limits trust
   138	  only DigitalOcean's overwritten `do-connecting-ip`; they never fall back to `X-Forwarded-For` or a header
   139	  owned by another provider. The bounded Fly fallback must instead set `fly`; `direct` is local-only.
   140	- Health checks on `/healthz` for both HTTP services.
   141	- Separate component-level environment variables and secrets.
   142	- Explicit instance size/count; no console-only drift.
   143	- Image release tag/digest recorded in deployment evidence.
   144	- Deployment-failure and domain-failure alerts.
   145	- TTL metadata and destruction job for staging.

   148	
   149	## 4. Database roles and connection variables
   150	
   151	Create four roles per database. The migration/owner credential is never injected into an App Platform runtime
   152	component.
   153	
   154	| Role | Runtime surface | Permissions | Connection mode |
   155	|---|---|---|---|
   156	| `migration` | CI deployment job only | DDL, migrations, grants, controlled queue provisioning | Direct; job-scoped secret |
   157	| `app` | `web` | Tenant RLS, application functions; `NOSUPERUSER NOBYPASSRLS` | Transaction pool for requests; separate direct connection for `LISTEN` |
   158	| `readonly` | `discovery` | `SELECT` only on `public_sessions` and approved public views; zero grant on canonical money/PII tables | Bounded transaction pool |
   159	| `app_worker` | `worker` | Narrow worker functions, pg-boss/outbox/projector operations | Direct/session connection |
   160	
   161	The application contract intentionally stays provider-neutral:
   162	
   163	| Variable | Component value |
   164	|---|---|
   165	| `DATABASE_URL` | `web`: pooled `app` URL; `discovery`: pooled `readonly` URL. Same key, different component-scoped secret. |
   166	| `DATABASE_URL_DIRECT` | `worker`: direct/session `app_worker` URL. |
   167	| `DATABASE_URL_LISTEN` | `web`: direct/session `app` URL used only by the SSE listener. |
   168	| `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION` | Deployment job only; absent from `web`, `discovery`, and `worker`. |
   169	
   170	There is **no mandatory `DATABASE_URL_REPLICA`**. If the trigger-based DO read-only node is purchased, replace
   171	only the `discovery` component's `DATABASE_URL` with that node's pooled `readonly` endpoint. Business code and
   172	the other components do not change. Authenticated post-write reads remain on the primary.
   173	
   174	Require TLS CA and hostname verification even on the private path. Trusted sources and least-privilege roles
   175	remain active; VPC routing is not a substitute for database authorization.
   176	
   177	---
   178	
   179	## 5. Secrets and KMS
   180	
   181	- Store runtime secrets as App Platform encrypted environment values, scoped to the component that consumes
   182	  each value. Never commit literal secrets or place them in an App Spec.
   183	- Keep `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION` in the protected CI environment/job only.
   184	- Continue the accepted application encryption contract: AWS KMS envelope encryption for staging/production,
   185	  local `ENCRYPTION_KEY` fallback only for local development. KMS is an application-level portability boundary,
   186	  not permission to expose plaintext secrets in App Platform.
   187	- Separate Google OAuth, Meta, Messenger, Zalo, SePay/ApiPay, session, KMS ciphertext, Sentry and webhook values
   188	  per environment.
   189	- Rotate session/auth/webhook secrets with an audited runbook; test fail-closed behavior when KMS decrypt fails.
   190	
   191	The current code still contains Telegram-era auth and requires `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`. Keep that temporary secret
   192	only until the DD-13/DD-37 Google/email replacement lands and the Telegram route/tests are removed.
   193	
   194	---

   196	## 6. Build, deploy and immutable promotion
   197	
   198	Required flow:
   199	
   200	```text
   201	pull request
   202	  → format + typecheck + lint + production build + full tests
   203	  → Semgrep CE code scan + OSV dependency scan
   204	  → build OCI image once
   205	  → sign image; record release tag + digest
   206	  → create/update ephemeral staging from IaC
   207	  → migration + queue provisioning job with DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION
   208	  → UAT/DAST/load/failover/PITR/provider-exit gates
   209	  → update production App Spec with the exact tested release
   210	  → verify running digest + health + money smoke
   211	```
   212	
   213	App creation/update is automated from reviewed specs:
   214	
   215	```bash
   216	# First create; use `doctl apps update` for later reconciliations.
   217	doctl apps create --spec infra/app.staging.yaml
   218	doctl apps create --spec infra/app.prod.yaml
   219	```
   220	
   221	The CI workflow supplies the immutable release value to both specs. Do not rebuild for production and do not
   222	promote a mutable tag.
   223	
   224	### Required CI order
   225	
   226	1. Validate target PostgreSQL version/extensions and roles.
   227	2. Run migrations with `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION`.
   228	3. Run `infra/entrypoint.sh provision` using the same job-scoped credential.
   229	4. Deploy/reconcile components.
   230	5. Wait for health/readiness; execute smoke and reconnect checks.
   231	
   232	Migration and queue provisioning must not be implemented as an App Platform runtime command that injects the DDL
   233	credential into all components.
   234

   236	
   237	## 7. URLs and channel configuration
   238	
   239	Use distinct domains and provider registrations:
   240	
   241	| Surface | Local/integration | Staging | Production |
   242	|---|---|---|---|
   243	| Google OAuth redirect | local/tunnel client | staging client + staging domain | production client + production domain |
   244	| Meta Login/Messenger | developer/test app or test users | staging app/page | live reviewed app/page |
   245	| Zalo | test club/bot | staging club/bot | per-club production tokens encrypted in DB |
   246	| SePay/ApiPay | sandbox/test account | staging account | production accounts/webhooks |
   247	| PWA/API base | localhost/tunnel | staging domain | production domain |
   248	
   249	Webhook updates are idempotent deployment steps. Verify the registered callback after every domain/app change; do
   250	not rely on a manual checklist entry without provider-side evidence.
   251	
   252	---
   253	
   254	## 8. Rollback and recovery
   255	
   256	### Application rollback
   257	
   258	- Select the previously verified immutable release in the App Spec and reconcile the app.
   259	- Confirm `web`/`discovery` health, SSE re-subscription, worker queue lag and no duplicate money mutations.
   260	- If a forward migration has run, do not execute destructive down-migrations. Use expand/contract and forward-fix.

docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-11-## 0. TL;DR
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-12-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:13:Deploy Plexco như **một modular monolith container-hoá**: **1 Docker image → 3 process group** (`web` · `discovery` · `worker`) cùng codebase, khác entrypoint/DB-role. Chạy trên **DigitalOcean App Platform** (compute) + **DO Managed Postgres HA-standby cùng VPC private**, **region Singapore** (DD-04 ✅ (c), 2026-07-16 — thay Fly; Fly = fallback (a); pre-build **egress-IP gate §7.1** — xem `../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`). Full infra spec (DO App Spec, supersede `fly.toml`) làm ở **build slice riêng**. Queue/cron nằm trên Postgres qua **pg-boss** (không Redis/Kafka). Realtime = **SSE fan-out qua Postgres `LISTEN/NOTIFY`** (không cần message broker).
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-14-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-15-**KHÔNG:** Kubernetes · tách microservices · serverless function cho worker/webhook/SSE · Redis cho queue.
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-16-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-17-Triết lý xuyên suốt: *PostgreSQL + domain model là canonical store; mọi surface là projection*. Deployment chỉ được phép **bảo toàn** bất biến đó, không tạo nguồn sự thật thứ hai.

docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-192-**Hai nguyên tắc mạng then chốt:**
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:193:1. **Postgres production đi qua VPC private theo DD-04 (c)** — DO App Platform và DO Managed PG co-locate cùng VPC; trusted sources + DB role least-privilege vẫn bắt buộc. **Không** mở public path âm thầm để lấy dedicated egress IP: nếu pre-build egress-IP gate không đạt, chuyển nguyên vẹn sang fallback (a) Fly + DO với TLS `sslmode=verify-full` + Fly static-egress-IP allowlist. docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-218-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-219-## 8. Backup/DR & Observability
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-220-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:221:**Backup/DR:** Managed HA + PITR + **encrypted offsite backup ngoài DO** để giảm concentration risk. **Restore drill:** DB cô lập → apply roles/policies → checksum schema → đối chiếu count (tenants, charges, transactions, allocations, outbox, audit). RPO/RTO được chốt bằng số đo sau provision, failover drill và restore drill; DD-04 provider choice đã đóng.
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-222-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-223-**Observability:** structured log gắn opaque `tenant_id`/`job_id` (không secret/PII) → metrics → traces → **SLO alert** (sync lag, reconcile p99, availability) → on-call + runbook link. Sentry cho error tracking (TDD §7).
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-224-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-225-**Runbook bắt buộc:** cross-tenant leak · public PII · wrong allocation · identity compromise · token leak · DB restore · provider outage · queue backlog · admin session compromise · marketplace abuse.
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-226-

docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:229:## 9. Cost estimate — Day-1 baseline + trigger-based upgrades (DD-04 Rev 2)
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-230-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:231:> **Model (thay Tier A/B cũ $121–133 / $202–254).** Day-1 chạy **baseline money-safe tối thiểu** `$120–135/tháng` — đã gồm non-negotiables: HA primary+standby, PITR, VPC private, fail-closed RLS, KMS envelope encryption, offsite-backup allowance, cùng **§9.3 read-isolation controls**. Các add-on đắt (read replica vật lý, WAF Pro, Sentry Team, worker thứ 2, resize DB) **KHÔNG bắt buộc Day-1** — bật theo **telemetry trigger đo được** (§9.2). Nguồn chuẩn: `../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md` §5.

docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-260-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-261-Discovery/public reads được cô lập khỏi money-path **ngay trên primary** bằng: **projection-only DB role** (zero grant trên bảng tiền/PII) · **bounded pool + statement_timeout** · **reserved connection budget** cho money-path · **CDN cache** cho public reads · **circuit-breaker** ngắt read khi primary áp lực. Read replica vật lý = upgrade Tier B khi contention đo được, **không** phải điều kiện money-safe Day-1. (record §5.3)
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-262-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-263-### 9.4 Ephemeral staging
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-264-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:265:Staging chạy đúng topology DO (VPC/failover/gate acceptance) nhưng **ephemeral** (spin-up cho drill rồi teardown): ~$8–18/tháng → tổng prod+staging **~$128–153/tháng**. (record §5.4)
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-266-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-267-### Tier C — Hardening một lần / định kỳ (không phải hosting cố định)
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-268-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-269-Image signing (CI), **DAST** trên staging, **load/soak** infra, **restore drill** định kỳ (~$10–30/drill), **pen-test** thuê ngoài, PDPA/GDPR + audit-retention tooling (§7, §8, §11). Phần lớn là **chi phí CI/người/định kỳ**, ước lượng riêng theo đợt — **không** gộp vào hosting hằng tháng.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-49-## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-50-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:51:### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-52-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:53:DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-54-table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:55:and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-56-`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-57-contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-58-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:59:| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-60-|---|---|---|---|---|
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:61:| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:62:| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-63-| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:64:| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-65-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-66-**Gate status: `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`** — SePay, ApiPay and Zalo Bot are `UNRESOLVED` (2026-07-18). No
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-67-overall result is recorded and the closure marker is intentionally absent: their official documentation never
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:68:affirmatively addresses outbound caller source-IP policy, and inference from omitted registration steps does
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-69-not meet this gate's evidence bar. The owner must obtain an attributable support or contractual response from
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-70-each of the three providers (for ApiPay: the DD-30 enterprise onboarding contract explicitly, not only the
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-71-BYO API) before this gate can close.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-72-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:73:- All four rows attributable and **NO** → record `Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` plus the closure marker.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:74:  That permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review only**; it does
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-75-  not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-76-- All four rows attributable and one or more **YES** → record `Overall result: FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED`. That
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:77:  blocks the DO App Platform + VPC green path and requires a separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:78:  fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-79-- Any row still missing, ambiguous, stale, or only inferred → gate status stays `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-80-  no overall result or closure marker may be recorded, even if another row is already an attributable `YES`.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-81-- Until the gate closes, do not create the App Spec and do not disable the private DB path silently.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-83-### 1.2 Repository preconditions
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-102-Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-103-Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-104-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-105-| Resource | Staging | Production |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-106-|---|---|---|
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:107:| App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-108-| App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:109:| VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-110-| PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:111:| DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-112-| Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-113-| Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-114-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:115:Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-116-ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-119-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:120:## 3. One image, three App Platform components
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-121-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-122-The App Specs deploy the same immutable image as three components. Use a unique release tag such as the git SHA,
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-123-resolve and record its registry digest, and prohibit mutable `latest` promotion.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-124-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-125-| Component | Day-1 production shape | Command | DB identity | Scaling contract |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-126-|---|---:|---|---|---|
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-127-| `web` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh web` | `app` pooled URL + `app` direct listener URL | Request-based RPS/p95 autoscaling is eligible on shared CPU; CPU autoscaling requires dedicated CPU |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-128-| `discovery` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh discovery` | `readonly` pooled URL on the primary at Day 1 | Bounded pool + timeout + CDN + circuit breaker; read-only node only after telemetry trigger |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-129-| `worker` | `1 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb-fixed` | `infra/entrypoint.sh worker` | `app_worker` direct/session URL | One durable consumer at Day 1; add a second scalable worker only after idempotency and queue-lag gates pass |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-130-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:131:App Platform high availability for the HTTP paths requires at least two containers, hence `web=2` and
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-132-`discovery=2`. The worker starts single by design; pg-boss durability is in PostgreSQL, not local disk.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-133-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-134-### Required App Spec controls
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-135-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:136:- VPC attachment to the environment's `sgp1` VPC; no dedicated egress-IP block on the VPC path.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-137-- Set `TRUSTED_INGRESS_PROVIDER=digitalocean` on every public HTTP component. Application rate limits trust
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-138-  only DigitalOcean's overwritten `do-connecting-ip`; they never fall back to `X-Forwarded-For` or a header
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:139:  owned by another provider. The bounded Fly fallback must instead set `fly`; `direct` is local-only.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-140-- Health checks on `/healthz` for both HTTP services.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-141-- Separate component-level environment variables and secrets.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-142-- Explicit instance size/count; no console-only drift.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-143-- Image release tag/digest recorded in deployment evidence.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-144-- Deployment-failure and domain-failure alerts.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-149-## 4. Database roles and connection variables
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-150-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:151:Create four roles per database. The migration/owner credential is never injected into an App Platform runtime
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-152-component.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-153-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-154-| Role | Runtime surface | Permissions | Connection mode |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-155-|---|---|---|---|
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-156-| `migration` | CI deployment job only | DDL, migrations, grants, controlled queue provisioning | Direct; job-scoped secret |
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-170-There is **no mandatory `DATABASE_URL_REPLICA`**. If the trigger-based DO read-only node is purchased, replace
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-171-only the `discovery` component's `DATABASE_URL` with that node's pooled `readonly` endpoint. Business code and
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-172-the other components do not change. Authenticated post-write reads remain on the primary.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-173-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-174-Require TLS CA and hostname verification even on the private path. Trusted sources and least-privilege roles
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:175:remain active; VPC routing is not a substitute for database authorization.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-179-## 5. Secrets and KMS
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-180-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:181:- Store runtime secrets as App Platform encrypted environment values, scoped to the component that consumes
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-182-  each value. Never commit literal secrets or place them in an App Spec.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-183-- Keep `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION` in the protected CI environment/job only.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-184-- Continue the accepted application encryption contract: AWS KMS envelope encryption for staging/production,
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-185-  local `ENCRYPTION_KEY` fallback only for local development. KMS is an application-level portability boundary,
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:186:  not permission to expose plaintext secrets in App Platform.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-187-- Separate Google OAuth, Meta, Messenger, Zalo, SePay/ApiPay, session, KMS ciphertext, Sentry and webhook values
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-188-  per environment.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-189-- Rotate session/auth/webhook secrets with an audited runbook; test fail-closed behavior when KMS decrypt fails.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-190-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-191-The current code still contains Telegram-era auth and requires `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`. Keep that temporary secret
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-227-2. Run migrations with `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION`.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-228-3. Run `infra/entrypoint.sh provision` using the same job-scoped credential.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-229-4. Deploy/reconcile components.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-230-5. Wait for health/readiness; execute smoke and reconnect checks.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-231-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:232:Migration and queue provisioning must not be implemented as an App Platform runtime command that injects the DDL
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-233-credential into all components.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-262-### Database recovery
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-263-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-264-- Provider PITR and HA are not sufficient alone. Maintain encrypted logical exports outside the DO account.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-265-- Quarterly, restore to vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema/role/RLS/sequence checks plus
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-266-  ledger, allocation, outbox and row-count reconciliation.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:267:- Rehearse the cross-provider flow and rollback semantics from DD-04 §7.4. Never dual-write money data during
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-268-  migration and never point back to a stale source after the target has accepted writes.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-271-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-272-## 9. Ephemeral staging lifecycle
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-273-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-274-Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-275-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:276:1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-277-2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-278-3. runs the selected gates;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-279-4. exports drill evidence;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:280:5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-281-6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-282-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-283-Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-284-silently become an always-on production mirror.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-288-## 10. Production acceptance checklist
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-289-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-290-This list mirrors the accepted record §7.6 and must be represented as executable checks/evidence in the infra
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-291-slice:
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-292-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:293:- [ ] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence — still open: §1.1 gate status
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-294-      `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE` (SePay/ApiPay/Zalo Bot unresolved, checked 2026-07-18).
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:295:- [ ] App Platform `sgp` is attached to the intended `sgp1` VPC.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:296:- [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only required private identities.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-297-- [ ] Four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener URLs work over the private route with TLS verification.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-298-- [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` pass rolling-deploy and health tests.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-299-- [ ] Discovery pool cap, `statement_timeout`, reserved money connection budget, CDN/rate limits and circuit breaker pass load tests.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-300-- [ ] Primary-failure drill proves DB failover and application reconnect.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-301-- [ ] SSE re-subscribes and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-302-- [ ] PITR and off-provider restores meet measured RPO/RTO and reconciliation gates.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-303-- [ ] KMS rotation and decrypt-failure paths pass.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-304-- [ ] Ephemeral staging reproduces from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:305:- [ ] Load evidence captures RPS/concurrency, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate, egress and projected cost.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:306:- [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal and asymmetric rollback pass DD-04 §7.4.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-307-- [ ] Budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup and KMS.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-311-## 11. Cost planning
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-316-| Ephemeral staging | `$8–18/month` average at 48–96h per release/drill |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-317-| Production Day 1 | **`$120–135/month`** |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-318-| Production + expected staging | **`$128–153/month`** |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-319-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-320-The production baseline includes two `web`, two `discovery`, one worker, DO PostgreSQL primary+standby,
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:321:Cloudflare Free, Sentry Developer, KMS, low-volume logs/offsite backup and egress allowances. Physical read-only
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-322-node, second worker, WAF Pro, Sentry Team and resizing are telemetry-triggered upgrades, not Day-1 requirements.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-323-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-324-Host count does not select a resource tier. Scale purchases require staging/production measurements and the
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:325:triggers in DD-04 §5.2.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-326-

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-328-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:329:## 12. Bounded Fly fallback
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-330-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:331:`infra/fly.toml` is retained only because DD-04 pre-authorizes fallback `(a)` when a production dependency proves
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:332:that fixed outbound IP is mandatory and App Platform+VPC cannot satisfy it.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-333-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-334-Activating fallback requires a recorded gate result and a separate reviewed App/DB network plan:
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-335-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:336:- compute on Fly with static egress;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:337:- DO PostgreSQL reached over public TLS with `sslmode=verify-full` and IP allowlist;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-338-- the same four DB roles, immutable image, migration-job boundary, backup/restore and acceptance gates;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-339-- a new cost reconciliation before procurement.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-340-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:341:Do not use the old “three Fly apps + Fly Postgres” topology. If fallback is not activated, no production Fly
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-342-resource is provisioned.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-343-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-344----
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-345-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-346-## 13. Anti-patterns
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-349-- Console-only production resources that IaC cannot reproduce.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-350-- Migration/owner credential in any runtime component.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-351-- Mutable image tags or rebuilding between staging and production.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-352-- Treating the HA standby as the discovery replica.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-353-- Buying a read-only node from host count rather than measured contention.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:354:- Disabling VPC to obtain a fixed egress IP without activating the approved fallback/design branch.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-355-- Treating provider PITR as the off-provider backup.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-356-- Leaving ephemeral staging alive without an owner and TTL.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-357-

docs/standards/decisions.md:135:- **Gate cứng (đã đạt qua (c)):** **auto-failover HA + uptime SLA + posture private-network** (VPC by construction).
docs/standards/decisions.md:136:- **QĐ-1 — posture network:** (a) public TLS + `sslmode=verify-full` + static-egress-IP allowlist [giữ Fly — **fallback**] · (b) PrivateLink AWS [rời Fly — loại] · **(c) co-locate compute+DB cùng cloud [đổi target Fly→DO, KHÔNG đảo DD-10] — ✅ ĐÃ CHỌN**.
docs/standards/decisions.md-137-- **QĐ-2 — ứng viên (theo posture a):**
docs/standards/decisions.md-138-  - **DigitalOcean Managed [nghiêng chọn]** — auto-failover standby + SLA 99.95% + PITR; bill phẳng, vận hành đơn giản; margin dự đoán chắc nhất (không nhất thiết rẻ nhất).
docs/standards/decisions.md:139:  - **Neon Scale** — HA multi-AZ **mọi plan**; SLA + PrivateLink ở Scale (PrivateLink **AWS-only → vô dụng từ Fly**); consumption floor 0.25 CU, trần = max-CU; rẻ hơn ở tải thấp nhưng cần benchmark.
docs/standards/decisions.md-140-  - **Supabase** — Pro và Team **rớt uptime-SLA gate**. Team từ `$599` có AWS PrivateLink nhưng không uptime SLA; SLA chỉ ở Enterprise custom, còn auto-failover phải contract-confirm. Không cost-comparable với finalist Day-1.
docs/standards/decisions.md-141-  - **Self-host / Railway-HA** — biến thể self-host (Patroni…), **không** Day-1 (hệ tiền + solo dev): tiết kiệm hạ tầng bị xoá bởi chi phí lao động/rủi ro.
docs/standards/decisions.md:142:- **Sửa dữ kiện so với lần thu hẹp trước:** (1) Neon HA **không** Scale-gated (multi-AZ mọi plan; Scale = SLA/PrivateLink/IP-allow); (2) Supabase Pro/Team không có uptime SLA; Team từ `$599` chỉ thêm PrivateLink, SLA ở Enterprise custom và auto-failover cần contract confirmation; (3) private-networking **chưa** được giải quyết bởi "Fly private networking" (khác cloud).
docs/standards/decisions.md-143-- **Tiêu chí:** auto-failover, SLA, posture network, RLS (Postgres core ở mọi provider), pooler/direct-conn cho pg-boss/`LISTEN`, chi phí *dự đoán được*, độ trễ Singapore, maintainability team nhỏ, **portability (chỉ Postgres thuần)**.
docs/standards/decisions.md-144-- **Hệ quả:** RPO/RTO chốt sau provision + restore drill; benchmark tải trước khi cam kết chi phí (đặc biệt Neon consumption). Không đổi domain model.

docs/standards/decisions.md-375-  1. **SI-1 — Pre-auth login-state CSRF binding:** cơ chế state token single-use/short-TTL/browser-bound (3 artifact mới: migration `auth_login_state`, route issue login-state, callback consume) — bổ sung cho (không thay thế) `sameOriginRequest()` hiện tại.
docs/standards/decisions.md-376-  2. **SI-3 — Explicit tenant-filter policy:** chốt làm **policy bằng văn bản** (mọi query tenant-scoped mới trên bề mặt auth/onboarding phải dùng CẢ `withTenant` LẪN predicate `tenant_id` tường minh) + **PORT code fix round-07** (`onboardingSnapshot`) từ v2 sang v3 (base `feat/phase1-fable5` không có).
docs/standards/decisions.md:377:  3. **KMS/secret source wiring (historical pre-merge gap):** **đã chốt 2026-07-06 (OQ-2): AWS KMS** cho staging/prod, dev local giữ fallback `ENCRYPTION_KEY`; runtime delivery qua encrypted-env boundary, không phụ thuộc Fly. Helper dùng chung đã land trong Auth v3; provider port Google/email phải reuse.
docs/standards/decisions.md-378-  4. **SI-2 — Keyed hash cho `account_identities.platform_user_hash`** (layer **web-auth**, KHÔNG phải `channel_identities.platform_user_lookup_hash` — hai cột khác tầng, xem bảng phân biệt trong invariant table §1): thay `sha256("telegram:"+id)` trần hiện tại (round-09 finding — brute-forceable vì TG user id entropy thấp, enumerable) bằng keyed HMAC; nguồn key đã chốt ở gap 3.

docs/standards/decisions.md-408-- **Bối cảnh:** Club Assistant đã chốt (DD-16..28) chỉ bao **KB per-CLB tenant-private**; trợ lý chỉ biết KB của đúng club. docs/standards/decisions.md-409-- **Quyết định (đề xuất):** Thêm plane **GLOBAL read model** `platform_kb_*` (`platform_kb_sources/documents/chunks`) — **KHÔNG có `tenant_id`**, do process **platform-admin** ghi, role `app` chỉ **`GRANT SELECT`** (cô lập-bằng-grant như DD-12/`readonly`, KHÔNG vào RLS list `0009`). Mở rộng `searchKnowledgeBase(tenantCtx, query)` thành **union 2 nhánh**: `kb_chunks` WHERE tenant (RLS) ∪ `platform_kb_chunks` (global), hợp nhất RRF, gắn nhãn scope; **precedence club > platform** khi trùng chủ đề (`bot_configs.include_platform_kb`/`platform_kb_weight`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-410-- **Quan hệ với DD-21 (extension, KHÔNG "nguyên vẹn"):** DD-34 **mở rộng nguồn grounding** của DD-21: grounding vẫn **chỉ KB**, nhưng **allowed KB sources** mở từ `kb_chunks(tenant)` → `kb_chunks(tenant) ∪ platform_kb_chunks`. **Live-context (DD-28) vẫn tách riêng, không trộn.** Khi DD-34 ✅ **phải patch DD-21** + Club Assistant specs (`feature_club_assistant.md` bất biến "KB prompt = chỉ `kb_chunks`" · `BE/feature_club_assistant_tech.md` §5) để phản ánh nguồn union — đây là điều kiện promote, không được để DD-21 mâu thuẫn.
docs/standards/decisions.md-411-- **Lý do:** Tri thức nền tảng dùng chung mọi club → 1 nguồn, cập nhật đồng loạt (không nhồi-trùng-lặp N club); có tiền lệ bảng global trong feature (`embedding_cache`).
docs/standards/decisions.md:412:- **Cô lập & guard (bảng SELECT cho mọi tenant nên phải chặt):** thiếu `tenant_id` chỉ ngăn **routing/association** — **KHÔNG** tự ngăn nội dung tenant bị ingest **nhầm** vào `content`. Vì `platform_kb_*` đọc-chung mọi tenant, `platform_kb_*` **KHÔNG ĐƯỢC chứa** (không phải "không thể chứa") dữ liệu tenant/PII ⇒ cần guard nhiều lớp: (1) **source allowlist** — chỉ ingest tài liệu sản phẩm Plexco (platform-docs-only), không nhận nguồn tenant; (2) **admin review** trước khi `status='active'`; (3) **scanner + test bất biến** assert `content` không chứa tenant identifier/PII pattern (SĐT/email/`pay_code`/tên roster). Grant: role `app` chỉ SELECT; ghi bởi role platform-admin.
docs/standards/decisions.md-413-- **Hệ quả:** Test bất biến: platform table không `tenant_id`; role `app` không ghi được; **scanner PII/tenant-id trên `content` = xanh**; union không rò KB club A↔B; câu hỏi nền tảng nhận chunk platform.
