 ## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
 
 ### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
 
-DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. Record
-written evidence for every production dependency before creating the App Spec:
+DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
+table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
+and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
+`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
+contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
 
-| Dependency | Required answer |
-|---|---|
-| SePay | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Inbound webhook verification alone does not imply that it does. |
-| ApiPay | Does enterprise provisioning restrict API callers by fixed source IP? |
-| Zalo Bot | Does the send API require fixed outbound IP? |
-| Meta Messenger | Does the Graph API require fixed outbound IP? |
-
-- All answers **no** → continue with App Platform + VPC.
-- Any answer **yes** → stop. Do not disable the private DB path silently. Activate the bounded Fly+DO
-  fallback or design a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
+| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
+| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
+| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
+
+**Gate status: `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`** — SePay, ApiPay and Zalo Bot are `UNRESOLVED` (2026-07-18). No
+overall result is recorded and the closure marker is intentionally absent: their official documentation never
+affirmatively addresses outbound caller source-IP policy, and inference from omitted registration steps does
+not meet this gate's evidence bar. The owner must obtain an attributable support or contractual response from
+each of the three providers (for ApiPay: the DD-30 enterprise onboarding contract explicitly, not only the
+BYO API) before this gate can close.
+
+- All four rows attributable and **NO** → record `Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` plus the closure marker.
+  That permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review only**; it does
+  not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning.
+- All four rows attributable and one or more **YES** → record `Overall result: FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED`. That
+  blocks the DO App Platform + VPC green path and requires a separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO
+  fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+- Any row still missing, ambiguous, stale, or only inferred → gate status stays `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`;
+  no overall result or closure marker may be recorded, even if another row is already an attributable `YES`.
+- Until the gate closes, do not create the App Spec and do not disable the private DB path silently.


 ## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
 
@@ -257,41 +273,42 @@ not rely on a manual checklist entry without provider-side evidence.
 
 Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
 
 1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
 2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
 3. runs the selected gates;
 4. exports drill evidence;
 5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
 6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
 
 Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
 silently become an always-on production mirror.

     1	# Autopilot slice — DigitalOcean App Platform + VPC deployment
     2	
     3	> **Slug:** `infra-do-app-vpc-deployment`  
     4	> **Status:** P0 manual design contract · **AUTOPILOT CODEGEN PROHIBITED**  
     5	> **Risk:** P0 · manual workflow only · no AP launcher/review loop  
     6	> **Canonical framework:** `autopilot-prompt-GENERIC` v0.6.0  
     7	> **Authority:** `dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md` Rev 2 + `standards/environments-setup.md` v2.1  
     8	> **Manifest:** `docs/autopilot-manifests/infra-do-app-vpc-deployment.json`
     9	
    10	## Policy gate
    11	
    12	Canonical v0.6.0 classifies scope touching migrations or secrets as P0. This design necessarily coordinates a
    13	one-shot migration and secret-bearing database/deploy configuration, so it must not be executed by unattended
    14	autopilot. `.claude/commands/ap-21-infra-do-app-vpc-deployment.md` is intentionally disabled.
    15	
    16	First close DD-04 §7.1 through the P2 docs-only `infra-egress-compatibility-evidence` slice. A compatible result
    17	does not lower this contract's risk tier; implementation still requires separately authorized manual slices.
    18	
    19	## Manual task authority
    20	
    21	```text
    22	Implement the first production-grade DigitalOcean deployment contract for Plexco without changing
    23	application/domain behavior.
    24	
    25	The green path is DigitalOcean App Platform + DigitalOcean Managed PostgreSQL primary/HA standby in
    26	Singapore on the same VPC. One immutable OCI image runs three components: web, discovery, and worker.
    27	Create reproducible production and ephemeral-staging App Specs plus an approval-gated deploy workflow.
    28	
    29	This is NOT permission to provision paid resources, change DNS/webhooks, upload secrets, or deploy to
    30	production. Stop at a reviewed, locally validated branch READY for founder merge and later execution.
    31	```
    32	
    33	## Negative scope
    34	
    35	- No application, domain, auth, money, RLS-policy, migration, or schema behavior changes.
    36	- No Kubernetes/DOKS, Terraform provider abstraction, new DB proxy, Redis, or second data store.
    37	- No permanent staging environment and no permanent read-only node Day-1.
    38	- No console-only configuration; every production control must be represented in App Spec/workflow/docs.
    39	- No secret value in git, logs, App Spec literals, runtime image, or runtime-wide migration env.
    40	- Do not modify provider-dependent business code because none should exist.
    41

    54	
    55	Before editing an App Spec, write evidence into the §1.1 table in `environments-setup.md` for SePay,
    56	ApiPay, Zalo, and Messenger: whether server-to-server calls require a fixed outbound source IP.

    61	  and report that the bounded Fly fallback must be re-sliced.
    62	- Missing/ambiguous provider evidence → fail closed with the same HALT; do not infer compatibility.
    63	- Never solve this gate by enabling dedicated egress and silently removing the private DB route.
    64	
    65	## Implementation contract
    66	
    67	### App Specs
    68	
    69	- `infra/app.prod.yaml`: app name `plexco-prod`, Singapore region, one immutable image digest/tag, and
    70	  explicit `web`, `discovery`, `worker` components using existing entrypoint roles.
    71	- `infra/app.staging.yaml`: same component/DB-role shape, isolated names/endpoints, smallest safe resources,
    72	  and TTL/destruction metadata consumed by the workflow. It must not point to production DB/secrets.
    73	- Web and discovery start with two instances; worker starts with one. Health/readiness and rolling-deploy
    74	  behavior must preserve webhook/SSE availability.
    75	- Document accurately: request-rate/p95 autoscaling works on shared or dedicated compute; CPU-based
    76	  autoscaling requires dedicated CPU. Do not promise an unsupported policy in the spec.
    77	
    78	### Network and DB boundaries
    79	
    80	- Bind app and Managed PostgreSQL to the same VPC/private route; trusted sources remain explicit.
    81	- Component-scoped `DATABASE_URL`: `app` for web, `readonly` for discovery. Day-1 discovery points to the
    82	  primary through a bounded pool; future read-only-node adoption changes this component value only.
    83	- `DATABASE_URL_LISTEN` exists only on web; `DATABASE_URL_DIRECT` exists only on worker.
    84	- `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION` exists only in the approved one-shot deploy job. It must never be an App
    85	  Platform app/component secret or runtime env.
    86	- No runtime role is owner/superuser/BYPASSRLS. TLS CA/hostname verification remains enabled even on VPC.
    87	
    88	### Deploy workflow
    89	
    90	- Add `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`; do not overload ordinary PR CI with production authority.
    91	- Use immutable build provenance. Staging deploy/verify precedes production promotion of the exact artifact.
    92	- Production requires the repository environment approval boundary.
    93	- Ordered one-shot DB preparation: run migration runner first, then existing `provision`/pg-boss setup.
    94	- Run smoke/role/RLS/public-projection/SSE reconnect/pg-boss no-duplicate checks before promotion.
    95	- Include explicit ephemeral-staging destroy and a safe TTL cleanup path.
    96	- Do not run the workflow or call paid/provider mutations in this slice.
    97	
    98	### Fly fallback file
    99	
   100	Keep `infra/fly.toml` as a clearly marked bounded fallback, not a competing primary deployment. It must say
   101	that activation requires a separate fallback slice with static egress, trusted-source allowlist, and
   102	`sslmode=verify-full`; it must not claim cross-cloud private networking.
   103	
   104	## Verification
   105	
   106	1. Parse both App Specs and workflow YAML with a deterministic validator.
   107	2. Run the current official non-provisioning DigitalOcean checks on **both** specs:
   108	   `doctl apps spec validate <spec-file>` (local/schema + remote correctness unless `--schema-only`) and
   109	   `doctl apps propose --spec <spec-file>` (review/validate/cost proposal). Record `doctl version`, output and date;
   110	   these commands validate/propose only — do not call `apps create/update` in this slice.
   111	3. Add static assertions that:
   112	   - no spec/workflow contains a literal secret;
   113	   - no runtime component contains `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION`;
   114	   - web/discovery/worker receive only their permitted DB variables;
   115	   - staging and production identifiers/endpoints cannot cross;
   116	   - migration precedes queue provisioning;
   117	   - production promotion has an approval environment and exact-artifact input.
   118	4. Start the throwaway local PostgreSQL service, require `DATABASE_URL_TEST` to point to `plexco_test`, then
   119	   run `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`, `pnpm -w typecheck`, `pnpm -w lint`, `pnpm -w test`, and
   120	   `pnpm -w build`. Never use a development, staging, or production database for the suite.
--- FINAL DECISION §7 ---
   235	  → switch DATABASE_URL* atomically
   236	  → restart/re-subscribe LISTEN/NOTIFY consumers
   237	  → resume worker and writes
   238	  → observe before decommissioning source
   239	```
   240	
   241	PostgreSQL logical replication does **not** replicate DDL/schema changes, sequence state, large objects,
   242	views, or roles/grants. Apply and verify those separately. During shadow validation the target is read-only;
   243	dual-writing money data is prohibited because it creates split-brain and makes rollback ambiguous.
   244	
   245	Cutover acceptance gates under the same production-shaped load:
   246	
   247	- zero lost or duplicated charge, transaction, allocation, outbox, or audit records;
   248	- public reads remain available; mutation pause is **≤5 minutes** in the rehearsal at Day-1 data size;
   249	- target error rate is no worse by more than `0.1` percentage point and p95/p99 latency is no worse by
   250	  more than `10%` versus the source benchmark;
   251	- target connection usage remains below `80%` of its safe connection budget and queue lag returns to SLO;
   252	- SSE listeners re-subscribe and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations;
   253	- DNS/connection TTL, credential rotation, and old-source isolation are proven in the runbook.
   254	
   255	Rollback is asymmetric:
   256	
   257	- **Before** the target accepts writes: switch connections back to the still-current source.
   258	- **After** the target accepts writes: never point back to a stale source. Use forward-fix, or prove reverse
   259	  replication and sequence/ledger reconciliation before rollback.
   260	- Keep the source read-only for at least **72 hours and one completed money-reconciliation cycle**, then
   261	  destroy it only after the observation gate passes.
   262	
   263	No provider migration can promise literally zero impact. The contract is bounded write interruption,
   264	uninterrupted public reads where practical, no split-brain, and no statistically material performance
   265	regression.
   266	
   267	### 7.5 Backup and disaster-recovery contract
   268	
   269	- Maintain three independent recovery forms: live HA data, provider PITR, and encrypted logical exports
   270	  outside the DigitalOcean account/provider.
   271	- Export schema, migrations, roles/grants, RLS, extensions, sequence state, and data; a data-only dump is
   272	  not a portable recovery set.
   273	- Run logical exports off-peak with bandwidth/concurrency caps and abort if production p95/p99 or queue
   274	  SLO regresses. At larger data sizes, use a temporary replica/target for the initial copy instead of paying
   275	  for a permanent monthly migration replica.
   276	- Quarterly, restore to temporary vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema checksum,
   277	  row-count, ledger/allocation/outbox reconciliation, smoke tests, and representative load tests.
   278	- Day-1 temporary portability/restore target allowance is approximately **`$10–30 per drill`** plus egress;
   279	  this is a drill cost, not an always-on monthly standby.
   280	
   281	### 7.6 Production acceptance checklist
   282	
   283	- [ ] §7.1 fixed-egress-IP gate is closed with written provider evidence.
   284	- [ ] App Platform is deployed in `sgp` and connected to the correct `sgp1` VPC.
   285	- [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only the required application private identity.
   286	- [ ] All four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener connection strings work on the private route with TLS
   287	      CA + hostname verification enabled.
   288	- [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` topology passes health-check and rolling-deploy tests.
   289	- [ ] Discovery pool caps, timeouts, rate limits, cache policy, and circuit breaker protect money pools.
   290	- [ ] Primary-failure drill proves automatic DB failover and application reconnection.
   291	- [ ] SSE listeners re-subscribe; pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
   292	- [ ] PITR and off-provider restore drills prove the selected RPO/RTO and reconciliation checks.
   293	- [ ] KMS envelope encryption, key rotation, and degraded/fail-closed paths are tested.
   294	- [ ] Ephemeral staging is reproducible from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
   295	- [ ] Load tests record concurrency, RPS, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate,
   296	      egress, and projected monthly cost before any scale purchase.
   297	- [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal passes §7.4, including rollback semantics and performance gates.
   298	- [ ] Monthly budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backups, and KMS.
   299

docs/standards/decisions.md-100-  - **DB lưu suffix trần UPPERCASE, 4 ký tự**, alphabet **A-Z + 2-9 trừ `0 O 1 I L`** (31, không phải Crockford); unique per-tenant; **immutable**; `CHECK (format + ≥1 chữ cái, loại toàn số)`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-101-  - **Prefix = bảng global `pay_prefix_registry(prefix PK, tenant_id, state current|retired, locked_at)` — nguồn DUY NHẤT, KHÔNG cột `tenants.pay_prefix`.** `UNIQUE one-current-per-tenant`; retired KHÔNG tái dụng (PK cho 1 tx thắng). **MVP: prefix IMMUTABLE vĩnh viễn sau code đầu tiên** (bỏ controlled-change — `memberships` chỉ lưu suffix nên đổi prefix sẽ resolve nhầm; tương lai cần thì phải có mapping `(membership_id,prefix,suffix,issued_at)`). **Choke-point atomic** phát-code ↔ đổi-prefix (`SELECT current-prefix FOR UPDATE` cùng lock order). Memo/QR `<PREFIX>-<CODE>`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-102-  - **Recognition = resolver `own | foreign | none`** (narrow SECURITY DEFINER, fail-closed — role `app` không blanket cross-tenant, `0009`). **full-issued ⟺ `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp** (retired-chưa-lock + suffix mới → `none`, không false-issued). Prefix BẮT BUỘC; regex thêm `-?`. **Memo-level precedence:** hard-token (foreign / own-prefix+unissued / dash-lạ / error / >1-candidate) → HARD unmatched (thắng name-fallback); ≥2 distinct own → unmatched; đúng 1 own → resolve; chỉ `none`(`PAYMENT`) → name-fallback.
docs/standards/decisions.md-103-  - **Allocation = INVARIANT** qua **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (pay_code chỉ resolve MEMBERSHIP; ledger quyết charge): auto `exact`/`over`/`under`(FIFO partial, edge #8) **+ unknown-fee-phía-sau (giữ phần chắc chắn)**; manual **unknown-fee-đầu**/`already-paid`; ≥2 code / provider-code-mismatch → unmatched.
docs/standards/decisions.md:104:  - **Provider-agnostic:** parser/generator/matcher ở core. **ApiPay = Phase-1 primary** (DD-30) — parse `data.content` (schema reverify khi build); **SePay = fallback**, `code` chỉ evidence; giữ webhook nhận tx không-code (không `skip_if_no_code`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-105-  - **Merge:** pay_code giữ trên tombstone; recognition **follow `merged_into_membership_id`** (tenant/status/cycle guards). `left`-còn-nợ nhận diện.
docs/standards/decisions.md:106:- **Propagation ĐÃ làm ở SPEC (2026-07-13):** `identity-model` §merge + `feature_identity_tech` (pay_code tombstone follow-through) ✅ · connector `feature_connector_onboarding.md` 4.3b + `_tech` M2 (ApiPay enterprise = Phase-1 **primary**) + **schema `provider_connections.auth_mode += 'enterprise'`** (CHECK shape đầy đủ per provider/auth_mode; platform credential từ KMS, không per-tenant) + **CO-OQ-6 đóng** (`skip_if_no_code=0`, adapter-only) ✅ · `feature_payment_reconcile_tech` **§4 worker pipeline** (`normalize→discover→resolve→precedence→providerCode-crosscheck→plan`) + `bank_transactions.provider_code_enc` + §8 fixtures ✅.
docs/standards/decisions.md-107-- **Propagation CÒN LẠI (khi build P4, code):** wire core + **thay tests** (impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts`); implement **`discoverPayCodeCandidates` + resolver narrow fn + `planIncomingAllocations()`**; migration **`pay_prefix_registry`** (one-current-per-tenant, choke-point) + `CHECK` trên `memberships.pay_code`.

docs/standards/decisions.md-123-## DD-04 — Hosting PostgreSQL · ✅ Accepted (2026-07-16) — Option (c) DO co-locate
docs/standards/decisions.md-124-
docs/standards/decisions.md-125-> ✅ **FINAL (2026-07-16, owner):** chọn **QĐ-1 (c) — co-locate compute + Postgres trên DigitalOcean** (App Platform + Managed PG HA-standby, **cùng VPC private**, Singapore). Supersede đề xuất (a) trong brief. Đổi deployment target **Fly → DO** (KHÔNG đảo DD-10 — App Platform chạy standalone container). Lý do: private DB by construction + managed HA/SLA/PITR + một provider (đơn giản cho solo dev) + fit `LISTEN/NOTIFY` session-pool (không scale-to-zero) + portability. Record đầy đủ: `../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-126->
docs/standards/decisions.md:127:> ⚠️ **Pre-build gate (§7.1) — CHƯA verify:** DO App Platform **không bật đồng thời** VPC-private-DB **và** dedicated egress-IP. **Phải xác minh** SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger có yêu cầu fixed outbound source-IP không **TRƯỚC khi dựng DO App Spec**; nếu có → dùng fallback **(a) Fly + DO**, **KHÔNG** âm thầm tắt private-DB path. Mitigation bắt buộc: **offsite encrypted backup + restore-to-another-Postgres runbook** (chống concentration risk). Cost planning (record Rev 2 — Day-1 baseline + trigger-based): **`$120–135/mo` Day-1 baseline** (2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker × 1 GiB + DO PG HA `$60.90` + Cloudflare Free + Sentry Developer + KMS `$1–3` + logs/R2 `$0–5` + egress `$0–8`) — **money-safe nhờ non-negotiables + read-isolation controls** (projection-role, bounded pool, reserved budget, CDN, circuit-breaker), KHÔNG cần read-replica/WAF-Pro/Sentry-Team Day-1. Add-on đó = **trigger-based upgrade** khi telemetry chạm ngưỡng. Ephemeral staging `$8–18` → prod+staging `$128–153`. **Caveat:** deferral chỉ an toàn khi read-isolation controls ship thật + trigger được giám sát. (thay số Tier `$202–254` cũ; chi tiết deployment-infra §9 + record §5)
docs/standards/decisions.md-128->

docs/standards/decisions.md-236-
docs/standards/decisions.md:237:## DD-15 — Zalo Bot `from.id` có ổn định xuyên nhiều bot khác nhau không · ⬜ Mở — SPIKE (2026-07-13) — `../dd-14-15-zalo-spike-analysis.md`
docs/standards/decisions.md-238-> **Spike xác minh Zalo** (gộp với DD-14). Không chặn an toàn identity: target luôn scope theo bot/OA endpoint cho tới khi bằng chứng provider cho phép nới. Kết quả spike chỉ có thể giảm friction/linking, không được làm điều kiện để tránh collision.
docs/standards/decisions.md-239-
docs/standards/decisions.md-240-- **Bối cảnh:** Plexco đa-bot (mỗi host/CLB một bot). Cùng một người ở hai bot khác nhau: `from.id` là id toàn cục theo user Zalo, hay scoped-per-bot? (Messenger PSID đã xác nhận page-scoped.)
docs/standards/decisions.md:241:- **Cần xác minh:** với Zalo Bot Platform.
docs/standards/decisions.md-242-- **Target fail-safe hiện hành:** normalize hai keyed HMAC domain-separated: `provider_scope_lookup_hash = HMAC("channel-scope:zalo:<bot_or_oa_id>")` và `platform_user_lookup_hash = HMAC("channel-user:zalo:<from.id>")`; uniqueness là `(platform, provider_scope_lookup_hash, platform_user_lookup_hash)`. Raw bot/OA id và `from.id` không trở thành authority key/plaintext lookup.
docs/standards/decisions.md-243-- **Hệ quả:** Nếu sau này chứng minh `from.id` ổn định xuyên bot, một migration/merge-plan được audit có thể giảm số lần link; không tự đổi uniqueness hoặc auto-merge bằng suy đoán. Nếu scoped-per-bot thì contract hiện tại đã đúng. `link_codes` bao ca xấu nhất (`00-start-here/identity-model.md §3`).

docs/standards/decisions.md:359:## DD-30 — Provider onboarding: APIpay enterprise auto-provision per-host · ✅ Chốt
docs/standards/decisions.md-360-
docs/standards/decisions.md:361:- **Bối cảnh:** Onboarding hiện tại (`feature_connector_onboarding`, Draft) = mỗi host **BYO** (SePay OAuth / ApiPay api_key). Để **launch nhanh**, dùng **quan hệ enterprise với APIpay** ở cấp platform để **auto-provision** webhook/bank-account cho từng host (khỏi bắt host tự OAuth/nhập key).
docs/standards/decisions.md-362-- **Quyết định (chốt):** Platform giữ **APIpay enterprise credential**; onboarding host = platform gọi APIpay enterprise API đăng ký **per-host** bank-account + webhook `/webhook/apipay/:token`. **Tiền vẫn về tài khoản ngân hàng RIÊNG của host** — **money-custody KHÔNG đổi**, giữ invariant `feature_payment_reconcile` ("platform không giữ tiền player"). Routing tenant vẫn theo `:token → bank_accounts.webhook_token → tenant_id` (**BL-PAY-6**).
docs/standards/decisions.md-363-- **Lý do:** Giảm ma sát onboarding (self-serve chậm/khó với host phổ thông); tận dụng enterprise để auto-register. Vì không đảo money-custody nên **không** đụng hard rule / rủi ro pháp lý trung gian thanh toán.
docs/standards/decisions.md-364-- **Hệ quả:**
docs/standards/decisions.md:365:  - `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (Draft — sửa được): thêm path **"APIpay enterprise auto-provision"** (platform credential, per-host account/webhook), giữ BYO như fallback; provider vẫn `apipay`; verify HMAC `ApiPay-Signature` per-host secret không đổi.
docs/standards/decisions.md-366-  - **Bảo mật:** enterprise credential platform = tài sản KMS; lộ = ảnh hưởng nhiều tenant → cần rotation + audit riêng. Cần điều khoản enterprise (giới hạn số account/webhook, phí) từ APIpay.
docs/standards/decisions.md-367-  - **KHÔNG** đụng ledger/reconcile core — chỉ đường onboarding/provisioning.
docs/standards/decisions.md-368-

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-1-# 💳 DD-02 Deep Analysis — `pay_code` (rev 6)
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-2-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:3:> **Mục đích:** chốt **contract pay_code** đúng với core đã có + provider-agnostic (ApiPay primary, SePay fallback).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:4:> **Ngày:** 2026-07-13 (rev 6) · **Canonical:** `packages/core/src/matching.ts` (`extractPayCode`/`countPayCodes`) · `packages/core/src/payments-logic.ts` (`classifyIncoming`/`classifyCoverage`) · `packages/db/schema.ts` (memberships/tenants) · `feature_payment_reconcile.md` (edge #8/#9) + `business-rules.md` (BL-PAY-2..7) · `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (ApiPay/SePay, CO-OQ-6) · `identity-model.md` (merge) · DD-30 · AUDIT.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-5-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-6-> ⚠️ **REV 6** (xem §0.1): (a) **1 nguồn prefix** = `pay_prefix_registry` (bỏ `tenants.pay_prefix`, one-current-per-tenant); (b) **prefix IMMUTABLE sau first issuance** + **choke-point atomic** phát-code↔đổi-prefix (§6.1); (c) **full-issued code định nghĩa hình thức** = `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp (§3.4) → retired-chưa-lock không tạo false issued; (d) memo-level precedence + `planIncomingAllocations()` (unknown-fee đầu vs sau).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-7-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-8----
--
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-22-  | **Chỉ `none`, không code evidence** (vd `PAYMENT`) | **name-fallback** |
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-23-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-24-  *Lặp cùng một own code hai lần = một distinct code. `none` chỉ dẫn tới name-fallback khi memo **không** chứa hard-token nào.*
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-25-- **Amount/allocation = INVARIANT:** pay_code chỉ **resolve MEMBERSHIP** (ai). **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (§4) dựng plan — **auto: `exact` / `over` / `under`(FIFO partial) / unknown-fee-PHÍA-SAU (giữ phần chắc chắn)**; **manual: unknown-fee-ĐẦU / `already-paid`**.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:26:- **Provider-agnostic:** parser/generator/matcher ở **Plexco core**. **ApiPay = Phase-1 primary** (DD-30 ✅) — parse `data.content` (không có field code bóc sẵn). **SePay = optional fallback** — `code` chỉ là **evidence bổ sung, KHÔNG canonical**; `code` ↔ content/prefix/tenant bất đồng → **fail-closed → unmatched**.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-27-- **Giữ webhook nhận cả giao dịch KHÔNG có code** (để name-based fallback chạy tiếp). **Không** bật `skip_if_no_code` ở tầng provider.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-28-- **Phase:** pay_code = **P4** (BL-PAY-3). `decisions.md` DD-02 **đã sửa P3→P4** (2026-07-13).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-63-| Merge: "re-parent" (l.186) vs "giữ trên merged" (l.188) mâu thuẫn | Merge contract **silent về pay_code** (`identity-model` step 6). docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:277:- SePay `code` là evidence; **core không được phụ thuộc** vào nó. `providerCode` có nhưng **không khớp** parser/content/prefix/tenant → **unmatched** (fail-closed).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:278:- Options như `is_verify_payment`, `skip_if_no_code` **chỉ thuộc SePay adapter**, **không** vào core DD-02. **Không** bật `skip_if_no_code` (sẽ khiến giao dịch thiếu code không tới Plexco, phá name-based fallback).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:279:- **`pay_prefix_registry`** là **canonical của Plexco**, KHÔNG phải "cấu hình SePay".
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:280:- ApiPay + SePay chạy **cùng fixtures** (§11).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-305-1. **Parser:** `payCodeRe` + `-?` + **`discoverPayCodeCandidates`** (enumerate mọi split, không loại `skipCodes` pre-resolve); **resolver narrow SECURITY DEFINER** `own|foreign|none` (full-issued = `locked_at NOT NULL` + `pay_code` match). Wire vào reconcile production.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-306-2. **Ledger:** **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (§4). `classifyCoverage` để dành combo.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-307-3. **Migration:** **`pay_prefix_registry`** (PK `prefix`, state, `locked_at`, one-current-per-tenant, **không** `tenants.pay_prefix`; choke-point atomic §6.1) + `CHECK` trên `memberships.pay_code` (format + `[A-Z]`) + **`bank_transactions.provider_code_enc`** (evidence) + **`provider_connections` CHECK shape đầy đủ per (provider, auth_mode)** (spec đã có).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-308-   > ⚠️ **Backfill guard (bắt buộc khi migrate):** query DB **thật** xem có `memberships.pay_code IS NOT NULL` không. Code search hiện **không thấy production writer**, nhưng nếu DB đã có dữ liệu → **HALT**: map prefix **thủ công** per-tenant + set `locked_at` cho từng prefix đã phát. **KHÔNG** suy prefix từ `slug` (sai namespace → resolve nhầm). CHECK/registry chỉ bật sau khi backfill xong.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:309:4. **Merge code:** recognition follow `merged_into_membership_id` (feature_identity merge path). **Provider-code cross-check** (SePay `provider_code` ↔ candidate/content/prefix/tenant → mismatch = hard-unmatched).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:310:5. **Provider verify (build):** verify webhook + dedupe (`UNIQUE(tenant,provider,external_id)` — BL-PAY-4) + amount, cho **cả** ApiPay lẫn SePay.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:311:   > ⚠️ **ApiPay header/payload schema chưa công khai đầy đủ** — trang chính thức mới xác nhận có webhook/API + "verify chữ ký nếu hỗ trợ". Field (`data.content`/`amount`/`transactionId`/`bankAccountPublicId`/`ApiPay-Signature`) §7 phải **REVERIFY khi build**. Positioning ApiPay-primary (DD-30) không đổi.

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-315-## 10. Quyết định (đã chốt) + việc thực thi
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-316-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-317-1. **Độ dài suffix = 4** (owner 2026-07-13) — contract duy nhất, không còn tuỳ chọn 5.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-318-2. **Prefix:** nguồn duy nhất = **`pay_prefix_registry`** (prefix PK, state, `locked_at`, **one-current-per-tenant**; **không** cột `tenants.pay_prefix`); retired không tái dụng. **Prefix IMMUTABLE sau first issuance**; **issuance và pre-lock change dùng CÙNG `FOR UPDATE` choke-point** (§6.1). Memo/QR `<PREFIX>-<CODE>`.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:319:3. **Bất biến:** recognition qua resolver **`own|foreign|none`** — **full-issued ⟺ `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp** (retired-chưa-lock + suffix mới → `none`; dash-token lạ → hard); **memo-level precedence** (hard-token thắng name-fallback; own-unissued/foreign/error/>1-candidate → HARD unmatched; ≥2 own → unmatched; đúng 1 → resolve; chỉ none → name-fallback); **allocation qua `planIncomingAllocations()`** (auto exact/over/under-partial + unknown-fee-phía-sau; manual unknown-fee-đầu/already-paid); provider-agnostic (ApiPay primary, SePay code = evidence); `left`-còn-nợ nhận diện; **`merged` → follow tombstone**.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:320:4. **Propagation:** SPEC **đã làm** (phase P4, connector ApiPay ✅ primary + `auth_mode='enterprise'` + CO-OQ-6 đóng, merge tombstone ở identity-model/_tech, reconcile §4/§8) — xem §9. **Còn lại = code/migration khi build P4** (parser/resolver/`planIncomingAllocations`/`pay_prefix_registry`).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-321-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-322-**Đã ghi canonical (2026-07-13):** `decisions.md` DD-02 → ✅ + phase P4 + changelog v1.22/v1.23; spec propagation (identity-model/_tech, connector .md/_tech + `auth_mode='enterprise'`, reconcile §4/§8) — xem §9. **Còn lại = code/migration khi build P4** (impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts`).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:326:## 11. Test fixtures bắt buộc (ApiPay + SePay cùng bộ)
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-327-`THANH TOAN` (không nhầm mã `THANH`) · **`PAYMENT` khi `PAY` KHÔNG own → `none` → name-fallback** · **`PAYMENT` khi `PAY` LÀ own current prefix → `MENT` unissued → HARD unmatched** · **own prefix + code UNISSUED `BMT7Q2B` → HARD unmatched** · **`BMTRANS` (skipCodes noise, unresolved squashed) → `none`** · **`BMT-RANS` (dash + skipCodes) → HARD** · **token trùng skipCodes nhưng full-issued → resolve theo ownership (issued thắng skipCodes)** · **foreign full-issued code trong memo (vd `... ABC7Q2A ...`, ABC là tenant khác đã lock) → discover + resolve → HARD unmatched** (không bỏ sót do chỉ anchor own-prefix) · `ABC-7Q2A` dash prefix lạ → hard-unmatched · bare code (reject deterministic) · no-token → name-fallback · **`BMT-7Q2A` + `BMT7Q2A` đều nhận** · provider/raw mismatch (→unmatched) · **lowercase / toàn-số DB insert → CHECK reject** · **`left` còn open charge → nhận diện** · `left` hết nợ → already-paid/unmatched · **`merged` → follow tombstone** (QR cũ chạy) · nhiều code (→unmatched) · `planIncomingAllocations`: exact→auto · over→auto+overpay · **under→auto FIFO partial** (charge `partial`, không notify paid) · **unknown-fee PHÍA SAU (100k known → unknown, trả 150k) → allocate 100k debt đầu, giữ 50k unallocated** · **unknown-fee ĐẦU → toàn bộ unmatched** · already-paid→unmatched · **retired prefix KHÔNG cấp lại** · **2 tenant claim cùng prefix đồng thời → PK cho đúng 1 tx thắng** · one-current-per-tenant enforce · **concurrent first-issuance vs prefix-change → đúng 1 thắng (§6.1)** · **đổi BMT→ABC TRƯỚC first issuance → phát `ABC-9K2P`: `BMT9K2P` (squashed, BMT `locked_at IS NULL`) = `none`/name-fallback, nhưng `BMT-9K2P` (dash) = HARD unmatched** · **retired prefix có `locked_at` (từng phát) + exact suffix → resolver công nhận** · >1 candidate → hard-unmatched · cross-tenant · dirty memo.

name: control-in-app-browser
description: "Control the in-app Browser for opening, navigating, inspecting visible or interactive page state, clicking, typing, screenshots, and local web testing. It can have existing signed-in sessions. For semantic operations on linked resources, prefer a purpose-built connector, API, or CLI when available."
---

# Browser
## Stop: choose the right surface before any browser action
Explicit browser intent wins: if the user names the in-app browser or Chrome, or asks to open, show, or navigate to a page; inspect its visual or interactive state; or interact with its UI, continue with Browser and do not substitute a connector.

Otherwise, treat a URL or open browser tab as context, not browser intent. Earlier Browser use does not make later semantic work browser-first. Before each semantic operation on a linked resource, you MUST query available and deferred tools for an applicable connector, API, or CLI. Reading this skill or scanning visible tools does not count. Do not initialize Browser for that operation until the query is complete. Use the non-browser tool when available. If it handles the current operation, continue the larger workflow without Browser for that operation. Use Browser when no such tool exists, the tool cannot access the resource or lacks a required capability, or UI work remains; use available browser context before asking the user to repeat it.

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

## Setup Documentation
Use `await agent.documentation.get("<name>")` when one of these setup topics applies:
- `bootstrap-troubleshooting`: read when browser setup succeeds but discovery or selection fails
- `chrome-troubleshooting`: read when Chrome extension setup, installation, or communication fails

## Bootstrap
These setup details are internal. User-facing progress updates should be less technical in nature. Never mention `Node REPL`, `node_repl`, `REPL`, JavaScript sessions, module exports, reading documentation, or loading instructions unless a user is asking for that exact information. If setup or recovery is needed, describe it naturally as connecting to the browser or retrying the browser connection.

The `browser-client` module is the core entry point for browser use, and is available under `scripts/browser-client.mjs` in this plugin's root directory. ALWAYS import it using an absolute path. IMPORTANT: If this path cannot be found, stop and report that this plugin is missing `scripts/browser-client.mjs`. NEVER use the built in `browser-client` library.

Run browser setup code through the Node REPL `js` tool. In this environment the callable tool id typically appears as `mcp__node_repl__js`. If it is not already available, use tool discovery for `node_repl js` without setting a result limit. You need the `js` execution tool: `js_reset` only clears state, and `js_add_node_module_dir` only changes package resolution. Do not call either helper while trying to expose `js`. If `js` is still not available, search again for `node_repl js` with `limit: 10`.

Initialize the runtime once per fresh Node session. If `agent.browsers` already exists, reuse it; do not import or initialize another browser runtime.

```js
if (globalThis.agent?.browsers == null) {
  const { setupBrowserRuntime } = await import("<plugin root>/scripts/browser-client.mjs");
  await setupBrowserRuntime({ globals: globalThis });
}
```

Once a browser connection is established, reuse its existing browser binding across later turns and do not reread this skill. Once you have read a browser's complete documentation, do not read it again unless you select a different browser.

A tab binding is separate from its browser binding. If a later turn reports that a tab is missing, stale, closed, or not part of the current browser session, discard that tab binding and obtain or create a fresh tab from the existing browser binding. An empty `browser.tabs.list()` or `browser.user.openTabs()` result is normal after tab cleanup and does not invalidate the browser binding. Never call `agent.browsers.get*` to recover a tab; only an explicit browser-disconnected error invalidates the binding.

## Browser selection
The scenarios below are for the initial browser selection only. Before calling any `agent.browsers.get*` method, reuse an existing `globalThis.browser`, `globalThis.iab`, or `globalThis.chrome` binding that already serves the task. A new user turn does not invalidate a browser binding or require another selection or documentation call.

Select the initial browser with exactly one of these scenarios, in the order
shown. An explicit request for the in-app browser or Chrome always wins over URL
selection. Never call `getForUrl()` when the user names a browser.

App-provided in-app-browser context is ambient UI state, not a user instruction to select or switch browsers. Only the text of the user's request can explicitly choose a browser.

Do not inspect browser cookies, local storage, profiles, passwords, or session stores. Browser discovery must remain read-only.

When authentication blocks requested browser navigation, do not replace it with web search, a search engine, another site, or another source merely to bypass sign-in.

### The user explicitly requests a browser
A plugin mention in the user's request explicitly names its browser.
`[@Browser](plugin://browser@openai-bundled)` names the in-app browser.
`[@Chrome](plugin://chrome@openai-bundled)`,
`[@chrome-internal](plugin://chrome-internal@openai-bundled)`, and
`[@chrome-dev](plugin://chrome-dev@openai-bundled)` name Chrome. Follow the
corresponding explicit-browser scenario below.

The in-app browser is available only when the Browser skill is listed for the session. If the user explicitly requests the in-app browser and that skill is available, use a distinct persistent binding and immediately read its complete documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.iab == null) {
  globalThis.iab = await agent.browsers.get("iab");
  nodeRepl.write(await iab.documentation());
}
```

If the user explicitly requests the in-app browser but the Browser skill is not available, report that the in-app browser is unavailable instead of substituting another browser.

Chrome is available only when the Chrome skill is listed for the session. If the user explicitly requests Chrome and that skill is available, use a separate persistent binding and immediately read its complete documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.chrome == null) {
  globalThis.chrome = await agent.browsers.get("extension");
  nodeRepl.write(await chrome.documentation());
}
```

If the user explicitly requests Chrome but the Chrome skill is not available, report that Chrome is unavailable instead of substituting another browser.

An explicit browser choice remains in force for the task. If authentication blocks the task in an explicitly selected browser, your next response must explicitly ask the user to sign in in that browser and tell you when it is ready, unless that browser's documentation provides a supported authentication flow to try first. Merely reporting that sign-in is required is not sufficient. Do not switch to another browser unless the user asks or approves the switch.

### The task requires browser interaction, the user does not specify a browser, and the task has a target URL
When the user supplies a URL or the intended URL can be reasonably inferred from the request, replace the example below with that URL and let browser-client choose the browser best suited to it:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.getForUrl("https://example.com/");
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

### The user specifies neither a browser nor a target URL
Use the runtime default, which prefers the in-app browser when it is available and otherwise uses Chrome. Do not list browsers first:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.getDefault();
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

## After setup
If setup succeeds but browser discovery or selection fails, read `await agent.documentation.get("bootstrap-troubleshooting")` before resetting the JavaScript session or trying another browser-control mechanism.

If the failure is specific to Chrome extension setup, installation, or communication, read `await agent.documentation.get("chrome-troubleshooting")` before retrying or taking another recovery action.

When the user did not explicitly choose a browser, a browser selected by the runtime is not a user constraint. Do not switch browsers based only on an assumption about authentication. If navigation shows that the selected browser lacks the required authentication, select another available browser before asking the user to sign in. You may select it without resetting the Node session. Preserve existing `iab`, `chrome`, and `browser` bindings when they are still useful. Existing tabs remain bound to the browser that created them. After selecting a different browser, obtain a tab from that browser before continuing and read its complete documentation.

The ability to interact directly with browsers is exposed through the `browser-client` runtime via the `agent.browsers.*` API. Before trying to interact with a selected browser for the first time, you MUST emit and read the complete documentation returned by its `documentation()` call in one go. For the initial documentation read, run the exact direct `nodeRepl.write(await <browser>.documentation());` call shown in the applicable scenario above. Do not assign the documentation to a variable, inspect its length, slice it, truncate it, summarize it, or emit only an excerpt. Do not proactively split the documentation into pages or chunks. Only if the tool output itself explicitly reports that it was truncated may you emit and read smaller chunks until you have read the documentation in its entirety.

Only the Node REPL `js` tool (`mcp__node_repl__js`) can be used to control the selected browser. Do not use external MCP browser-control tools, separate browser automation servers, or other browser skills for this surface. References to Playwright mean the in-skill `tab.playwright` API after browser-client setup.

     1	# Feature: Connector Onboarding (Zalo · SePay · ApiPay) — Auto-Register Upgrade
     2	
     3	> **Canonical phase:** P3 (payment ingest) · P4 (web onboarding) · P4.7 (Zalo) — *cross-cutting upgrade*
     4	> **Track:** 🔌 Connector / Onboarding
     5	> **Version:** 0.9 · 2026-07-17
     6	> **Status:** Draft (standard provider contracts reviewed; ApiPay enterprise provider gate open)
     7	> **Prerequisites:** P3 webhook ingest stable · P4 onboarding wizard scaffold
     8	> **Tài liệu liên quan:** `feature_web_auth_onboarding.md` (nâng cấp step bank/zalo/messenger) · `feature_zalo_channel.md` · `feature_player_messenger.md` · `feature_payment_reconcile.md` · `../channels/zalo/README.md` §6–7 · `../standards/decisions.md` DD-30/DD-37/DD-33 · `../product/TDD.md` · `../standards/business-rules.md` BL-PAY-6, BL-SEC-1/2, BL-TEN-1, BL-ID-6, BL-SEC-5 · `../research/player-side-saas-AUDIT.md` §B2
     9	
    10	> **Authority DD-65 (2026-07-17):** doc này vẫn cover connector Zalo/SePay/ApiPay. Messenger central Page do platform vận hành; FB Login + Page riêng là **optional tenant connector**, không chặn tenant readiness. Chi tiết ở `feature_player_messenger.md` + `feature_web_auth_onboarding.md`.
    11	
    12	---
    13	
    14	## 0. TL;DR — vấn đề & đề xuất
    15	
    16	**Vấn đề (hiện trạng spec):** Cả ba connector đều bắt **chủ CLB (non-tech)** tự tay làm bước khó nhất:
    17	
    18	| Connector | Owner đang phải làm tay (theo spec hiện tại) |
    19	|---|---|
    20	| Zalo | Tạo bot → dán token → **tự vào Zalo platform dán webhook URL** `{api_base}/webhook/zalo/{token}` (`channels/zalo/README.md` §6) |
    21	| SePay | Nhập credential → **tự vào SePay dashboard tạo webhook** trỏ về Plexco (`feature_web_auth_onboarding` step bank) |
    22	| ApiPay | Nhập credential → **tự vào ApiPay dashboard tạo webhook** |
    23	
    24	Bước "copy URL → mò vào dashboard bên thứ ba → dán đúng chỗ → set trigger/secret" là điểm rơi onboarding lớn nhất cho non-tech user, và cũng là nguồn lỗi "webhook drift".
    25	
    26	**Đề xuất:** Đảo nguyên tắc — **owner chỉ xác nhận tài khoản nhận tiền hoặc cấp credential thuộc về họ; Plexco tự đăng ký webhook bằng API**. Mức độ chắc chắn (kiểm chứng dashboard/docs thật ngày 2026-06-20):
    27	
    28	- **SePay** — ✅ *verified*. Có **OAuth2** (scopes `bank-account:read`, `transaction:read`, `webhook:read|write|delete`) + `POST /api/v1/webhooks` (contract đầy đủ — §4.2) + `GET /api/v1/transactions` + **giả lập giao dịch** → "Kết nối SePay" 1 nút, không dán token.
    29	- **ApiPay** — **phase-1 primary = enterprise auto-provision (DD-30):** host không nhập platform credential; Plexco provision bank-account/webhook riêng cho host, tiền vẫn về TK host. API-key BYO qua `GET /v2/client/banks` + `POST /v1/client/webhooks` là fallback explicit. Standard webhook API đã verified; request shape/terms của enterprise phải được xác nhận trước production.
    30	- **Zalo** — 🟡 *candidate, blocked by sandbox confirmation (CO-OQ-1)*. Pattern token + `getMe`/`setWebhook` xuất hiện trong các SDK Zalo Bot cộng đồng, **nhưng chưa kiểm chứng endpoint/tham số chính thức** trên tier Zalo Bot mà CLB dùng. **Không** đưa vào triển khai cho tới khi confirm sandbox; trước đó giữ luồng nhập tay hiện có.
    31	
    32	**Kết quả:** 3 flow rối → 1 pattern "Kết nối" thống nhất, kết thúc bằng tín hiệu xanh "đã nhận giao dịch/tin nhắn thử". SePay/ApiPay standard webhook auto-register bỏ bước dán URL; enterprise ApiPay chỉ bật sau khi provider contract chứng minh request shape + replay safety; Zalo theo sau khi gỡ block.
    33	
    34	### Quan hệ với spec hiện tại
    35	
    36	Đây là **bản nâng cấp**, không thay thế:
    37	
    38	- `feature_web_auth_onboarding.md` — step `bank_connect`/`bank_verify` và `zalo_optional`: từ "manual portal config" → **auto-register + verify-by-event**.
    39	- `channels/zalo/README.md` §6 — giữ manual webhook cho tới khi gỡ CO-OQ-1; sau đó mới chuyển sang **Plexco gọi `setWebhook`**. §7 drift từ "alert" → **auto re-register** khi API chính thức được xác nhận.
    40	- `feature_payment_reconcile.md` — phần ingest giữ nguyên; chỉ thay đường *thiết lập* webhook.
    41	
    42	### Nguồn kiểm chứng provider (refresh 2026-07-17)
    43	
    44	| Provider | Nguồn chính | Đã chốt trong spec | Còn phải sandbox |
    45	|---|---|---|---|
    46	| SePay | `https://developer.sepay.vn/en/sepay-webhooks/xac-thuc`, `https://developer.sepay.vn/en/sepay-oauth2/api-webhook` | OAuth Webhooks API; production default `authen_type=HMAC_SHA256`; headers `X-SePay-Signature` + `X-SePay-Timestamp`; signed bytes `{timestamp}.{raw_body}`; API-key fallback exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>` | sandbox status/error/retry mapping; auth header/signing contract đã chốt |
    47	| ApiPay | standard/BYO docs: `https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication`, `https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/banks`, `https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/webhooks`; DD-30 là authority cho primary path | Standard/BYO: `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`; banks `/v2/client/banks`; webhooks `/v1/client/webhooks`; `ApiPay-Signature` HMAC raw body | enterprise request/response + exact replay mechanism + terms/SLA; standard sandbox status/error mapping |
    48	| Zalo | `channels/zalo/README.md` + SDK/community references | tenant routing + secret verify pattern | endpoint/tham số chính thức `getMe`/`setWebhook`/`getWebhookInfo` trên tier CLB (CO-OQ-1) |
    49	
    50	---
    51	
    52	## 1. Mô tả
    53	
    54	Cung cấp một trải nghiệm "Kết nối" (Connect) thống nhất cho 3 connector. ApiPay phase-1 dùng enterprise auto-provision nên host chỉ chọn/xác nhận TK nhận tiền, không nhập platform credential; SePay dùng OAuth consent; Zalo và ApiPay-BYO fallback mới nhận credential thuộc về owner. Việc đăng ký webhook về Plexco và xác minh hoạt động do hệ thống tự làm.
    55	
    56	### Bất biến
    57	
    58	- **B1 — Routing theo opaque token, KHÔNG theo nội dung payload.** Tenant luôn suy ra từ `:token` trong path → `*.webhook_token → tenant_id`, rồi mới verify `account_fingerprint`/account khớp. Ref: **BL-PAY-6**, **BL-ID-6**.
    59	- **B2 — Secret at-rest qua KMS, write-only.** Mọi token/secret (`bot_token_enc`, `webhook_secret_enc`, OAuth tokens, PKCE verifier) mã hoá envelope (AES-GCM); không log; không trả lại sau khi lưu; có reset path. Ref: **BL-SEC-1**.
    60	- **B3 — Verify webhook timing-safe trên RAW body.** Sai chữ ký/timestamp → 401, không persist payload nghiệp vụ, security log không chứa secret. SePay production dùng HMAC-SHA256 trên `{unix_timestamp}.{raw_body}`, timestamp freshness ±300s và constant-time compare. API key chỉ explicit fallback/sandbox. Ref: **BL-SEC-2**, AUDIT B-webhook.
    61	- **B4 — RLS fail-closed.** Mọi bảng tenant-scoped ENABLE + FORCE RLS theo `app.tenant_id`. Riêng `oauth_transactions` là bảng bootstrap trước-tenant cho OAuth callback, không đọc trực tiếp qua tenant RLS; chỉ truy cập qua SECURITY DEFINER function lookup-by-state single-use. Ref: **BL-TEN-1**.
    62	- **B5 — Tự động hoá KHÔNG nới lỏng bảo mật.** Auto-register chỉ chạy trong context tenant đã auth (TenantOwner), idempotent, chịu rate limit **BL-SEC-5**.
    63	- **B6 — Auto-register là tiện ích, không phải điểm chết.** ApiPay enterprise không khả dụng/không phủ bank → hiện CTA **BYO API key fallback**; provider webhook API vẫn fail mới tới rescue nhập URL tay. Không tự downgrade `auth_mode` hoặc chuyển sang BYO mà chưa có hành động rõ ràng của owner.
    64	
    65	---
    66	
    67	## 2. Nguyên tắc thiết kế chung — "Connect" pattern
    68	
    69	### 2.1 Credential-minimization
    70	
    71	Owner chỉ đưa đúng **một thứ chỉ họ mới có**; mọi thao tác cấu hình webhook là việc của Plexco:
    72	
    73	| Connector | Owner đưa gì | Plexco tự làm | Bước tay còn lại (không bỏ được) |
    74	|---|---|---|---|
    75	| SePay ✅ | OAuth2 consent (nút "Đồng ý") | `GET /api/v1/bank-accounts` → `POST /api/v1/webhooks` *hoặc* poll `/transactions` | — (chỉ bấm Đồng ý) |
    76	| ApiPay ✅ — phase-1 primary (DD-30) | Không nhập credential; platform dùng enterprise credential để provision per-host | Tạo/bind bank account riêng của host + webhook | Chọn/xác nhận TK ngân hàng của chính host theo flow provider |
    77	| ApiPay BYO — fallback | accessKey + secretKey | `GET /v2/client/banks` → `POST /v1/client/webhooks` | Copy 2 key từ ApiPay → Plexco |
    78	| Zalo 🟡 | Bot token (per-CLB) | `getMe` → `setWebhook` → store enc *(sau khi gỡ CO-OQ-1)* | Tạo bot trong Zalo Bot Creator |
    79	
    80	> Zalo còn 1 bước tạo bot vì Zalo **không** có API tạo bot (owner tự tạo trong Zalo Bot Creator). SePay là "rảnh tay" nhất nhờ OAuth2.
    81	
    82	### 2.2 Capability-driven UI
    83	
    84	Một interface chung, UI render theo `auth_mode` của provider — thêm provider mới = khai báo capability, không viết lại màn hình.
    85	
    86	```typescript
    87	type AuthMode =
    88	  | { kind: "oauth2"; scopes: string[] }                      // SePay
    89	  | { kind: "api_token"; fields: ["apiToken"] }               // SePay outbound fallback
    90	  | { kind: "enterprise"; tenantCredentialFields: [] }         // ApiPay primary; platform KMS credential
    91	  | { kind: "api_key"; fields: ["accessKey", "secretKey"] }   // ApiPay BYO fallback
    92	  | { kind: "bot_token" };                                     // Zalo
    93	
    94	interface ConnectorProvider {
    95	  id: "zalo" | "sepay" | "apipay";
    96	  auth: AuthMode;
    97	  validate(creds: Credentials): Promise<ProviderIdentity>;      // getMe / OAuth /me / banks
    98	  autoRegisterWebhook(ctx: TenantCtx, creds): Promise<WebhookHandle>;
    99	  verify: { kind: "wait_inbound" } | { kind: "wait_transaction" } | { kind: "simulate" };
   100	}
   101	```
   102	
   103	### 2.3 Verify 2 tầng (chốt: kết hợp register-OK + event thật)
   104	
   105	```
   106	Tier 1 — "Đã kết nối"  := validate() OK  &&  autoRegisterWebhook() OK   (wiring kỹ thuật xong)
   107	Tier 2 — "Đã xác minh" := nhận event thật đầu tiên qua webhook          (chứng minh end-to-end)
   108	```
   109	
   110	- **Tier 1** = trạng thái `registered`. UI hiện "✅ Đã kết nối — đang chờ tín hiệu thử".

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-170-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-171-| # | Category | Case | Xử lý |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-172-|---|----------|------|-------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-173-| 1 | Security | Tenant suy từ nội dung payload | ❌ NEVER — chỉ `:token`→`webhook_token`, rồi verify `account_fingerprint` (BL-PAY-6) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:174:| 2 | Security | HMAC verify trên JSON re-serialize | Dùng **raw body** (§4.5); SePay signed input còn gồm exact timestamp prefix; test payload unicode/space lạ |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-175-| 3 | Security | Forged/unknown webhook token | 401, verify secret timing-safe, không persist (BL-SEC-2) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-176-| 4 | Security | `state` giả/replay ở OAuth callback | `OAUTH_STATE_INVALID`; state single-use, TTL 10', thiếu state → từ chối |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-177-| 5 | Security | Secret/token rò ra log | KMS at-rest, redaction; chỉ lưu `last_error_code` (BL-SEC-1) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-178-| 6 | Concurrency | 2 lần "Kết nối" đồng thời | `UNIQUE(tenant,provider)` + upsert; chỉ 1 webhook đăng ký |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-179-| 7 | Concurrency | Event đến trước khi `registered` commit | Chỉ `connecting` + ciphertext được verify; hợp lệ → CAS `connecting→active`. Register finalization CAS `connecting→registered` và preserve `active`; thiếu ciphertext/`error|revoked` reject |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-180-| 8 | Concurrency | Replay event nhà cung cấp | Flip Tier 2 idempotent; ingest dedup `(tenant,provider,external_id)` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-181-| 9 | Data Integrity | Provider tạo webhook OK nhưng DB commit fail | Lưu `provider_webhook_id` cùng transaction; lệch → job `connectors/cleanup-orphan-webhook` gọi `DELETE` webhook mồ côi |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:182:| 10 | Data Integrity (ApiPay) | TK `PENDING`/`manualActivationPending` | Không register; `webhook_status='connecting'`; job `connectors/recheck-pending-bank` (≤24h) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-183-| 11 | Data Integrity | Drift — owner đổi URL ở dashboard | `security/webhook-drift` (hourly) → auto re-register; fail → `drift`/alert |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:184:| 12 | Token lifecycle | SePay refresh token hết hạn/refresh fail | `status='error'` + nhắc kết nối lại; **không** ảnh hưởng connection/TK khác |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:185:| 13 | Token lifecycle | ApiPay BYO key bị thu hồi bên ngoài | Delivery fail liên tục → `error` + alert; runbook rotate (enterprise credential có platform-level rotation/audit riêng) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-186-| 14 | Cross-Feature | Gỡ connector | revoke OAuth / `DELETE webhook` → `status='revoked'`; **giữ** lịch sử `bank_transactions` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-187-| 15 | Cross-Feature | `BANK_ALREADY_BOUND` (fingerprint trùng tenant khác) | 409, không bind; tránh route nhầm tenant |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-188-| 16 | Abuse | Spam `*/connect` dò key | Rate limit BL-SEC-5 (§3) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:189:| 17 | Security | SePay HMAC stale/future timestamp, bad signature hoặc missing header | 401 trước parse/persist; no auth downgrade |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:190:| 18 | Security | SePay API-key fallback header sai | Chỉ exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>` constant-time; reject bare/custom header |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-232-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:233:- `bankSelectionRef` là reference opaque, TTL ngắn, do server cấp sau flow host chọn/xác nhận TK theo cơ chế enterprise đã được ApiPay phê duyệt; nó bound với tenant + owner + bank selection. Nó **không** phải accessKey/secretKey, platform credential, số TK thô hay provider webhook secret. Nếu enterprise contract thực tế không cung cấp được reference tương đương, HALT production mapping và xác nhận lại provider request shape; không tự mở input PII/secret mới.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-234-- Request schema `additionalProperties=false`: reject mọi field credential/secret ngoài shape trên. `consent` phải đúng `true`; resolve ref + authorization + TTL trước provider I/O.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:235:- Handler lock logical `(tenant_id, provider='apipay')`, upsert `provider_connections.auth_mode='enterprise'`, rồi provision/bind bank + webhook. Provider-side replay phải dùng **một cơ chế đã được enterprise contract chứng minh**: stable derived idempotency key nếu provider hỗ trợ, hoặc exact lookup/reuse bằng deterministic provision ref. Không có cơ chế nào → trả `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE`, không gọi create mù sau timeout. Enterprise credential chỉ đọc từ platform KMS, không lưu vào tenant row, response, analytics hay log.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-236-- Provider success + DB commit fail đi qua orphan cleanup §4.2; retry cùng key phải reuse provider resource/response, không tạo bank/webhook thứ hai. Cùng key nhưng body hash khác → `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` trước provider call.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:237:- Tenant đã có compatible enterprise binding → trả resource hiện hữu. Muốn đổi bank hoặc đổi `enterprise ↔ api_key` phải qua replace/revoke flow explicit; endpoint không silently downgrade sang BYO.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:238:- Enterprise unavailable/unsupported chỉ trả error có type để UI hiện CTA **"Dùng ApiPay key riêng"**; BYO endpoints không được gọi tự động.

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-277-

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-279-|----------|-------------|-----|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-280-| `*/webhooks/register` | ✅ | `(tenant_id, provider, provider_bank_ref)` — đã có `provider_webhook_id` thì reuse |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-281-| `apipay/provision` | ✅ | required `(tenant_id, route, Idempotency-Key)` + request hash + persisted canonical response; provider replay dùng verified idempotency key hoặc exact deterministic lookup/reuse; thiếu cả hai thì path fail-closed unavailable; same key/different body → 409 |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:282:| `apipay/connect` (BYO), `sepay/oauth/start` | ✅ | upsert `(tenant_id, provider)` |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-284-| `zalo/connect` 🟡 | ✅ | `(tenant_id)` — `zalo_bots` UNIQUE(tenant) |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-286-| Flip Tier 2 | ✅ | CAS `UPDATE ... WHERE status IN ('connecting','registered') RETURNING`; emit analytics only when one row transitions. `active` is idempotent; `drift` is processed without clearing drift; `error|revoked` reject |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-321-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:322:### 4.3 OAuth state/PKCE (SePay)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-323-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-324-- `start`: sinh `state` (≥128-bit) + `code_verifier`/`code_challenge` (S256), lưu `oauth_transactions` (TTL 10') qua `create_oauth_transaction(...)`, redirect authorize.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:325:- `callback`: top-level GET từ SePay → **không** có cookie `sameSite=strict`. Gọi SECURITY DEFINER `consume_oauth_transaction(state)` (search_path cố định, `SELECT FOR UPDATE`, TTL check, `DELETE` single-use) → `tenant_id` + `code_verifier`; exchange `authorization_code`+verifier → token; sau đó `SET LOCAL app.tenant_id` và ghi tenant-scoped bình thường; rotate/khôi phục session rồi về wizard.

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-362-### 4.6 Timeout/Expiry Spec
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-363-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-364-| Variant | Giá trị | Lý do |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-365-|---------|---------|-------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-366-| `oauth_transactions` TTL | 10 phút | chống replay, đủ cho consent |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:367:| SePay token refresh ngưỡng | còn < 5 phút | tránh gián đoạn webhook/poll |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-368-| Drift cron | 1 giờ | theo §7 hiện hữu |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:369:| ApiPay pending recheck | 30 phút, dừng sau 24h | manual activation ≤ 24h |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-370-| Provider API call | timeout 10s, 1 retry (backoff) | provider chậm, tránh treo onboarding |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-447-### Rollout (khớp product §11)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-448-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:449:1. **M1** — receiver verify 2 tầng + SePay/ApiPay raw-body HMAC; SePay freshness/no-downgrade; flip `active` khi event đầu.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:450:2. **M2** — **ApiPay ENTERPRISE auto-provision per-host = Phase-1 PRIMARY (DD-30 ✅ · DD-02)**: platform giữ 1 credential enterprise, tự provision per-host (không bắt host tự lấy `api_key`); BYO `api_key` là fallback. Xử lý bank `PENDING`. *(rủi ro thấp nhất, giao trước)*
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:451:3. **M3** — SePay OAuth2 (cần partner CO-OQ-2) + state/PKCE + refresh job; fallback token mode; tùy chọn poll `/transactions`.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-452-4. **M4** — Zalo auto `setWebhook` *(chỉ sau khi gỡ CO-OQ-1)*: land KMS backfill-or-disable/drop migration, state+ciphertext decision table and early-event CAS tests before receiver enablement; then drift auto re-register.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-453-5. **M5** — dọn fallback, telemetry, ẩn dần luồng nhập tay.

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-460-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-461-- [ ] Schema/RLS/indexes + forward migration `zalo_bots` áp dụng: KMS-backfill-or-disable legacy rows, enforce encrypted secret, drop plaintext `secret`, không dual-read.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-462-- [ ] Receiver/state decision table pass: only allowed status + ciphertext verifies; early event CAS cannot be downgraded by register finalization; analytics emits once.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-463-- [ ] Verify webhook timing-safe **trên raw body** (test re-serialize fail).
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:464:- [ ] SePay production register dùng `HMAC_SHA256`; verify `{timestamp}.{raw_body}`, ±300s, `sha256=` format và no-downgrade. API-key fallback chỉ exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>`.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:465:- [ ] `/api/onboarding/apipay/provision` là ApiPay phase-1 primary: request/response secret-free, ref bound tenant+owner, durable idempotency qua crash/retry, platform credential chỉ ở KMS.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:466:- [ ] Enterprise unavailable/unsupported trả typed error và chỉ **mời** BYO; không tự gọi BYO hoặc đổi `auth_mode`.

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-470-- [ ] Tenant isolation (RLS) + `BANK_ALREADY_BOUND` pass.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-471-- [ ] Provider API fail → fallback, không chặn onboarding (B6).

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-14-## 0. TL;DR — vấn đề & đề xuất
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-15-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-16-**Vấn đề (hiện trạng spec):** Cả ba connector đều bắt **chủ CLB (non-tech)** tự tay làm bước khó nhất:
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-17-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-18-| Connector | Owner đang phải làm tay (theo spec hiện tại) |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-19-|---|---|
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-20-| Zalo | Tạo bot → dán token → **tự vào Zalo platform dán webhook URL** `{api_base}/webhook/zalo/{token}` (`channels/zalo/README.md` §6) |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:21:| SePay | Nhập credential → **tự vào SePay dashboard tạo webhook** trỏ về Plexco (`feature_web_auth_onboarding` step bank) |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:22:| ApiPay | Nhập credential → **tự vào ApiPay dashboard tạo webhook** |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-23-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-24-Bước "copy URL → mò vào dashboard bên thứ ba → dán đúng chỗ → set trigger/secret" là điểm rơi onboarding lớn nhất cho non-tech user, và cũng là nguồn lỗi "webhook drift".
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-25-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-26-**Đề xuất:** Đảo nguyên tắc — **owner chỉ xác nhận tài khoản nhận tiền hoặc cấp credential thuộc về họ; Plexco tự đăng ký webhook bằng API**. Mức độ chắc chắn (kiểm chứng dashboard/docs thật ngày 2026-06-20):
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-27-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:28:- **SePay** — ✅ *verified*. Có **OAuth2** (scopes `bank-account:read`, `transaction:read`, `webhook:read|write|delete`) + `POST /api/v1/webhooks` (contract đầy đủ — §4.2) + `GET /api/v1/transactions` + **giả lập giao dịch** → "Kết nối SePay" 1 nút, không dán token.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:29:- **ApiPay** — **phase-1 primary = enterprise auto-provision (DD-30):** host không nhập platform credential; Plexco provision bank-account/webhook riêng cho host, tiền vẫn về TK host. API-key BYO qua `GET /v2/client/banks` + `POST /v1/client/webhooks` là fallback explicit. Standard webhook API đã verified; request shape/terms của enterprise phải được xác nhận trước production.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-30-- **Zalo** — 🟡 *candidate, blocked by sandbox confirmation (CO-OQ-1)*. Pattern token + `getMe`/`setWebhook` xuất hiện trong các SDK Zalo Bot cộng đồng, **nhưng chưa kiểm chứng endpoint/tham số chính thức** trên tier Zalo Bot mà CLB dùng. **Không** đưa vào triển khai cho tới khi confirm sandbox; trước đó giữ luồng nhập tay hiện có.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-31-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:32:**Kết quả:** 3 flow rối → 1 pattern "Kết nối" thống nhất, kết thúc bằng tín hiệu xanh "đã nhận giao dịch/tin nhắn thử". SePay/ApiPay standard webhook auto-register bỏ bước dán URL; enterprise ApiPay chỉ bật sau khi provider contract chứng minh request shape + replay safety; Zalo theo sau khi gỡ block.

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-45-|---|---|---|---|

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-50----
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-51-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-52-## 1. Mô tả
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-53-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:54:Cung cấp một trải nghiệm "Kết nối" (Connect) thống nhất cho 3 connector. ApiPay phase-1 dùng enterprise auto-provision nên host chỉ chọn/xác nhận TK nhận tiền, không nhập platform credential; SePay dùng OAuth consent; Zalo và ApiPay-BYO fallback mới nhận credential thuộc về owner. Việc đăng ký webhook về Plexco và xác minh hoạt động do hệ thống tự làm.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-55-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-56-### Bất biến
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-57-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-58-- **B1 — Routing theo opaque token, KHÔNG theo nội dung payload.** Tenant luôn suy ra từ `:token` trong path → `*.webhook_token → tenant_id`, rồi mới verify `account_fingerprint`/account khớp. Ref: **BL-PAY-6**, **BL-ID-6**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-59-- **B2 — Secret at-rest qua KMS, write-only.** Mọi token/secret (`bot_token_enc`, `webhook_secret_enc`, OAuth tokens, PKCE verifier) mã hoá envelope (AES-GCM); không log; không trả lại sau khi lưu; có reset path. Ref: **BL-SEC-1**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:60:- **B3 — Verify webhook timing-safe trên RAW body.** Sai chữ ký/timestamp → 401, không persist payload nghiệp vụ, security log không chứa secret. SePay production dùng HMAC-SHA256 trên `{unix_timestamp}.{raw_body}`, timestamp freshness ±300s và constant-time compare. API key chỉ explicit fallback/sandbox. Ref: **BL-SEC-2**, AUDIT B-webhook.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-61-- **B4 — RLS fail-closed.** Mọi bảng tenant-scoped ENABLE + FORCE RLS theo `app.tenant_id`. Riêng `oauth_transactions` là bảng bootstrap trước-tenant cho OAuth callback, không đọc trực tiếp qua tenant RLS; chỉ truy cập qua SECURITY DEFINER function lookup-by-state single-use. Ref: **BL-TEN-1**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-62-- **B5 — Tự động hoá KHÔNG nới lỏng bảo mật.** Auto-register chỉ chạy trong context tenant đã auth (TenantOwner), idempotent, chịu rate limit **BL-SEC-5**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:63:- **B6 — Auto-register là tiện ích, không phải điểm chết.** ApiPay enterprise không khả dụng/không phủ bank → hiện CTA **BYO API key fallback**; provider webhook API vẫn fail mới tới rescue nhập URL tay. Không tự downgrade `auth_mode` hoặc chuyển sang BYO mà chưa có hành động rõ ràng của owner.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-64-

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-70-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-71-Owner chỉ đưa đúng **một thứ chỉ họ mới có**; mọi thao tác cấu hình webhook là việc của Plexco:
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-72-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-73-| Connector | Owner đưa gì | Plexco tự làm | Bước tay còn lại (không bỏ được) |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-74-|---|---|---|---|
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:75:| SePay ✅ | OAuth2 consent (nút "Đồng ý") | `GET /api/v1/bank-accounts` → `POST /api/v1/webhooks` *hoặc* poll `/transactions` | — (chỉ bấm Đồng ý) |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:76:| ApiPay ✅ — phase-1 primary (DD-30) | Không nhập credential; platform dùng enterprise credential để provision per-host | Tạo/bind bank account riêng của host + webhook | Chọn/xác nhận TK ngân hàng của chính host theo flow provider |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:77:| ApiPay BYO — fallback | accessKey + secretKey | `GET /v2/client/banks` → `POST /v1/client/webhooks` | Copy 2 key từ ApiPay → Plexco |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-78-| Zalo 🟡 | Bot token (per-CLB) | `getMe` → `setWebhook` → store enc *(sau khi gỡ CO-OQ-1)* | Tạo bot trong Zalo Bot Creator |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-79-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:80:> Zalo còn 1 bước tạo bot vì Zalo **không** có API tạo bot (owner tự tạo trong Zalo Bot Creator). SePay là "rảnh tay" nhất nhờ OAuth2.

docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-13-

docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:15:Webhook (opaque token → bot/tenant) → Verify provider secret
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-16-    → Normalize stable event/user IDs → Update dedup
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-17-    → Identity/Domain Service (shared) → Outbox → Delivery
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-18-```
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-19-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:20:Bot-per-club model: webhook path token resolves bot endpoint/default tenant. Provider payload is NOT tenant authority. Identity lookup uses domain-separated HMACs for bot/OA scope and `from.id`; target tenant/action still re-authorize separately.
