docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-22----
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-23-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-24-## 2. Database Schema + Edge Cases (BE)
--
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-28-```sql
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-29-CREATE TABLE zalo_bots (
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-30-  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-31-  tenant_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES tenants(id) UNIQUE,
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:32:  bot_token_enc text NOT NULL,          -- KMS/envelope ciphertext, never plaintext
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:33:  webhook_token text NOT NULL UNIQUE,  -- random opaque route lookup; not a provider secret
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-34-  webhook_secret_enc bytea,            -- KMS/envelope ciphertext; narrow decrypt only
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-35-  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'connecting'
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-36-    CHECK (status IN ('connecting','registered','active','drift','error','revoked')),
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-37-  auto_registered boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
--
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-59-ALTER TABLE zalo_reply_contexts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-60-ALTER TABLE zalo_reply_contexts FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-61-```
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-62-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:63:`webhook_token` chỉ là random opaque locator trong URL; nó không thay cho provider verification secret. Handler resolve row theo token dưới narrow endpoint resolver, decrypt `webhook_secret_enc` qua KMS, so sánh header bằng constant-time compare rồi xóa secret khỏi memory sớm nhất có thể. Migration forward thực hiện theo thứ tự: add nullable encrypted column → application/KMS backfill legacy `secret` → connector không backfill được chuyển `error` và bắt owner reconnect → add/validate CHECK buộc mọi row có thể nhận webhook (`registered|active|drift`) phải có ciphertext → drop legacy `secret`. `connecting` có thể giữ NULL trong khi setup nhưng chỉ được nhận traffic sau khi ciphertext tồn tại; verified early event CAS `connecting→active`, và register finalization phải preserve `active`. `error|revoked` không nhận traffic. Không có dual-read plaintext sau cutover.
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-64-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-65-### Edge Cases (Backend)
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-66-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-67-| # | Category | Case | Xử lý |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-68-|---|----------|------|-------|
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:69:| 1 | Security | Tenant from message content | ❌ NEVER — webhook token/bot binding only |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-70-| 2 | Data Integrity | Stale number (list expired/changed) | Reject — "Danh sách đã thay đổi" |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-71-| 3 | Data Integrity | Number out of range | "Số không hợp lệ" |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-72-| 4 | Security | Forged/unknown webhook | Reject — narrow KMS decrypt + timing-safe verify; no plaintext fallback |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-73-| 5 | Concurrency | Replayed event | Update dedup — idempotent |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-74-| 6 | Concurrency | Concurrent number replies last slot | Shared service locks session: first active, second waitlisted/no-charge/no-venue |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:75:| 7 | Security | Bot token in logs | ❌ NEVER — KMS encrypted, log redaction |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-76-| 8 | Data Integrity | Webhook drift (URL changed) | Hourly `getWebhookInfo` — alert on drift |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-77-| 9 | Security | DM reachability cross-club/bot | Scoped exact `channel_identity_id` bound bot/OA; independent; target tenant re-authorized |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-78-| 10 | Resilience | Provider rate-limit/outage | Degrade — no canonical corruption |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-79-| 11 | Security | Free numeric message without context | NEVER execute join — require active context |
--
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-123-Before resolving Person, compute `provider_scope_lookup_hash=HMAC("channel-scope:zalo:"+bot_or_oa_id)` and `platform_user_lookup_hash=HMAC("channel-user:zalo:"+from.id)`. Lookup uniqueness is the three-part channel key; never use raw `from.id` alone or assume it is global across bots (DD-15).
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-124-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-125-### Token Operations
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-126-
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:127:- Envelope encrypt bot token and webhook verification secret (KMS); persist ciphertext only.
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-128-- UI: masked display only.
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-129-- Rotation: store pending credential version in KMS → verify → atomic promote → revoke old; `rotation_pending` is metadata, never a plaintext staging field.
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-130-- Drift monitor: hourly `getWebhookInfo` compare expected URL.
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-131-- Kill switch: privileged, owner-authenticated, audited.

docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-162-| 22 | Rejoin after refund | Existing `left`, refund complete | Shared core starts canonical new obligation lifecycle; adapter renders result |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-163-| 23 | Rejoin disposition unknown | Existing `left`, correction core cannot prove state | `REJOIN_REQUIRES_REVIEW`; player/charge/allocations unchanged |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-164-| 24 | Webhook secret persistence | Connect/rotate Zalo credential | DB/log/API contain ciphertext/redacted metadata only; no raw header or plaintext secret |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md-165-| 25 | Legacy secret cutover | Legacy row can/cannot be KMS-backfilled | Encrypt then drop plaintext; unprovable row becomes `error`; receiver accepts only `connecting|registered|active|drift` with ciphertext |
docs/features/BE/feature_zalo_channel_tech.md:166:| 26 | Early event vs registration | Signed event arrives after `setWebhook` but before register finalization | Event CAS sets `active`; finalization writes metadata without downgrading; analytics once |

docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-17-
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md:18:- Webhook token/bot binding xác định tenant — **KHÔNG BAO GIỜ** từ message content.

docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-42-### Edge Cases
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-43-
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-44-| # | Category | Case | Hành vi bắt buộc |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-45-|---|----------|------|-------------------|
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md:46:| 1 | Security | Tenant resolved từ message content | ❌ NEVER — webhook token/bot binding only |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-47-| 2 | Data Integrity | Stale number reply (list expired/changed) | Reject — "Danh sách đã thay đổi, gõ /join để xem lại" |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-48-| 3 | Data Integrity | Number out of range | "Số không hợp lệ — chọn từ 1 đến N" |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-49-| 4 | Security | Unsupported message type | Safe fallback — KHÔNG expose error details |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md:50:| 5 | Security | Bot token invalid/revoked | Degrade gracefully — KHÔNG corrupt canonical state |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-51-| 6 | Security | Webhook drift (URL changed) | Monitor + alert — owner notification |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-52-| 7 | Security | DM reachability cross-club | ❌ NEVER — club A's bot ≠ club B's reachability |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-53-| 8 | Resilience | Rate-limit / provider outage | Degrade without corrupting state — retry with backoff |
docs/features/feature_zalo_channel.md-54-| 9 | Security | Venue/roster/debt exposed before auth | ❌ NEVER — same person-self/privacy rules across Zalo/Messenger/web |

docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:193:1. **Postgres production đi qua VPC private theo DD-04 (c)** — DO App Platform và DO Managed PG co-locate cùng VPC; trusted sources + DB role least-privilege vẫn bắt buộc. **Không** mở public path âm thầm để lấy dedicated egress IP: nếu pre-build egress-IP gate không đạt, chuyển nguyên vẹn sang fallback (a) Fly + DO với TLS `sslmode=verify-full` + Fly static-egress-IP allowlist. docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-18-**Có HAI quyết định, phải chốt theo thứ tự:**
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-19-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-20-**QĐ-1 (lịch sử gate): posture private-network** — khi compute còn ở **Fly.io**, mọi managed DB nằm ở cloud khác nên **không có** private networking thật trừ khi PrivateLink (Supabase Team/Ent + AWS VPC) hoặc **co-locate compute vào cùng cloud DB**. Ba posture đã xét:
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md:21:- **(a) Public TLS + `sslmode=verify-full` + static-egress-IP allowlist** — giữ Fly, DB provider allowlist Fly egress IP. Đơn giản, giữ kiến trúc hiện tại. Câu chữ cũ ở `deployment-infra.md §6` ("Postgres KHÔNG bao giờ expose public") từng mâu thuẫn với posture này; rev 3 đã sync baseline thành "không public *unauthenticated*; truy cập qua TLS verify-full + IP allowlist".
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-22-- **(b) Strict private (PrivateLink/VPC)** — buộc chuyển deployment target khỏi Fly (vd sang AWS + Neon PrivateLink, hoặc co-locate DO). Đây là thay đổi hạ tầng, **KHÔNG đảo DD-10** vì DD-10 chỉ pin standalone container/không Vercel.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-23-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-24-**QĐ-2 (provider), phụ thuộc QĐ-1:**
--
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-54-4. **Read isolation** cho discovery/public reads ngay Day-1; read-only node vật lý chỉ bật khi bounded pool/cache/circuit-breaker không đủ và telemetry chạm trigger.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-55-5. **HA = auto-failover** (không chỉ "warm standby") + **PITR** — vì hệ tiền. ⬅️ **gate cứng**.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-56-6. **Uptime SLA** cam kết — hệ tiền cần cam kết hợp đồng, không chỉ best-effort. ⬅️ **gate cứng**.

    49	## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
    50	
    51	### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
    52	
    53	DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
    54	table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
    55	and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
    56	`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
    57	contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
    58	
    59	| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
    60	|---|---|---|---|---|
    61	| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
    62	| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
    63	| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
    64	| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
